Motivation: 现有反事实图像生成方法依赖生成对抗网络或扩散模型等辅助模型，用另一个黑盒来解释分类器，难以区分分类器的决策过程与生成器的归纳偏差，导致解释不够直接和透明。

Method: 提出一个无需生成模型的反事实生成框架：直接从分类器中提取因果证据来构建反事实图像。该方法具有确定性、无需额外模型训练，并允许在用户指定的感兴趣区域内进行受控编辑。

Result: 在真实世界医学影像数据集上的实验表明，所提方法成功改变分类器预测的同时，生成的图像比生成式基线更接近原始图像，提供了对分类器决策边界更直接和透明的视角。

Conclusion: 该工作为医学图像分类器的可解释性审计提供了一种更透明、直接且无需额外生成模型的新范式，避免了"用黑盒解释黑盒"的问题。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance in medical image diagnosis, yet their deployment in clinical settings remains constrained by limited explainability. Counterfactual images provide one means of auditing model behavior by showing how an image would need to change for a classifier to produce a different prediction. Existing approaches typically generate such explanations using auxiliary models, including generative adversarial networks and diffusion models. While often capable of producing visually realistic images, these methods explain one black-box model using another, making it difficult to separate the classifier's decision-making process from the inductive biases of the generator.
  We propose a novel counterfactual-generation framework that requires no generative model. Instead, counterfactuals are constructed directly from causal evidence extracted from the classifier. The resulting approach is deterministic, requires no additional model training, and enables controllable edits within user-specified regions of interest. Experiments on real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method successfully changes classifier predictions while remaining closer to the original image than generative baselines, providing a more direct and transparent view of the classifier's decision boundary.

</details>


### [103] [A Top-Down Framework for Metric-Scale Athlete Localization from Single Broadcast Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02705)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Linh-Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单帧广播画面中实现运动员公制尺度世界坐标定位的top-down框架，核心包括边界感知自适应分块和适配RTMPose-X的双关键点估计器，在公开测试集上达到97.44的LocSim分数和0.9128的mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单帧广播画面中进行运动员世界坐标定位面临超高清图像中极端尺度差异带来的内在挑战，现有方法在极限尺度变化下存在严重的召回率下降问题，需要一种轻量且无需架构修改的解决方案。

Method: 方法包含三项核心贡献：(1) Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling，一种基于语义的切片推理扩展，通过基于粗边界框预测迭代扩展切片边界，确保完整物体包含，缓解边界分割伪影且无需架构修改；(2) 将RTMPose-X适配为双关键点估计器（骨盆和地面投影点），并采用针对该几何耦合点对优化的Gated Attention Unit；(3) 通过相机标定射线投射将2D地面投影确定性提升至世界坐标。

Result: 在公开测试集上达到97.44的LocSim分数和0.9128的mAP，较基线提升超过21%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将极端尺度方差问题与透视畸变问题解耦处理，建立了针对高分辨率尺度变化的鲁棒解决方案，展示了在单帧广播画面中实现精确运动员定位的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate world-coordinate localization of athletes from single-frame broadcast footage is inherently challenging due to extreme scale disparities in ultra-high-resolution imagery. In this paper, we propose a top-down framework for metric-scale athlete localization from a single calibrated frame. Our approach centers on three key contributions. First, we propose Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling, a semantics-guided extension of standard sliced inference. By iteratively expanding tile boundaries based on coarse bounding-box predictions, it systematically ensures full object containment, effectively mitigating boundary-splitting artifacts through a lightweight pipeline adaptation without architectural modifications. By substantially mitigating recall degradation under extreme scale variance, Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling enables us to isolate perspective distortion as the primary source of residual localization error. Second, we adapt the RTMPose-X architecture into a specialized two-keypoint estimator (pelvis and ground projection), employing a reformulated Gated Attention Unit optimized for this geometrically coupled point pair, and then deterministically lift the 2D ground projections into world coordinates via camera-calibrated ray casting. On the public test set, our method achieves a LocSim score of 97.44 and an mAP of 0.9128, outperforming the baseline by over 21 \% and establishing a robust solution for high-resolution scale variance.

</details>


### [104] [MV-dVRK: A Multi-Viewpoint Benchmark for Spatial Surgical Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02717)
*Guido Caccianiga,Sergey Prokudin,Yutong Chen,Bernard Javot,Rachael L'Orsa,Omer Burak Aladağ,Yarden Sharon,Jens Rolinger,Ivan Capobianco,Anton Deguet,Siyu Tang,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-dVRK，这是首个结合多曝光同步立体视角、精确表面几何与相机位姿的离体手术数据集，并系统比较了不同稀疏多视角3D重建方法在内镜图像上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏多视角3D重建在大规模训练和优化技术下有了很大进步，但从未在真实内镜图像上被严格评估。临床遥操作机器人仅使用单立体相机，导致多视角数据极为稀缺，缺乏合适的基准来评估这些方法在外科场景中的实际表现。

Method: 构建MV-dVRK数据集：静态子集提供密集SfM参考几何（经工业3D扫描仪验证）、真实相机位姿和稀疏视角测试集；动态子集包含十个覆盖多种手术任务、运动学复杂度递增和软组织变形的序列。基于该数据系统比较零样本单目、立体、多立体和多视角3D重建方法。

Result: 使用两套内镜时，多立体重建覆盖最高；增加第三个视角后，基于优化的多视角方法最佳，在1mm容差下覆盖67%的地面真实表面点并恢复高精度相对相机位姿；而前馈基础模型在相同设置下仅覆盖43%。

Conclusion: MV-dVRK为外科多视角3D重建提供了首个严谨的评估基准，表明优化方法在稀疏多视角内镜重建中仍优于前馈基础模型，并揭示了多视点外科感知的研究方向。

Abstract: Large-scale training and refined optimization techniques have greatly improved sparse multi-view 3D reconstruction. Despite their relevance to surgery, such methods have never before been rigorously evaluated on real endoscopic images. Current clinical telerobots deploy a single stereo camera inside the patient, making multi-viewpoint data extremely rare. This paper presents MV-dVRK, the first ex-vivo surgical dataset to combine multiple exposure-synchronized stereo viewpoints with accurate surface geometry and camera poses. The static subset of the benchmark provides dense SfM reference geometry, validated against an industrial 3D scanner, together with ground-truth camera poses and sparse-view test sets. We use MV-dVRK to systematically compare zero-shot monocular, stereo, multi-stereo, and multi-view 3D reconstruction methods as the number of viewpoints increases. With two endoscopes, multi-stereo reconstruction achieves the highest coverage. With a third viewpoint, optimization-based multi-view methods perform best, covering 67% of ground-truth surface points within a 1 mm tolerance and recovering highly accurate relative camera poses. By contrast, feed-forward foundation models cover only 43% of the ground-truth surface in the same setting. MV-dVRK also includes ten dynamic sequences spanning multiple surgical tasks, with increasing kinematic complexity and tissue deformation, providing a basis for future research in multi-viewpoint surgical perception. The project is available at: https://mv-dvrk.is.mpg.de.

</details>


### [105] [RVSD: Retrieval Vision Sparse Decoding for Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02731)
*Canjie Liu,Jiawen Kang,Jinbo Wen,Zishao Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RVSD（检索视觉稀疏解码）框架，一种无需训练、即插即用的解码方法，首次在同一解码过程中统一了令牌稀疏化与语义空间视觉检索（SSVR），以在降低计算开销的同时缓解大视觉语言模型中的视觉幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在视觉语言任务中表现出色，但仍易产生视觉幻觉（VHs），严重影响其在真实应用中的可靠性。现有解决方案通常依赖精选数据集、额外训练或多轮解码，导致可观的计算开销，亟需一种高效且无需训练的缓解方法。

Method: RVSD包含两大核心机制：(1) 语义导向的令牌选择策略，选择性稀疏化冗余令牌同时保留关键视觉信息；(2) 语义空间视觉检索（SSVR）机制，将视觉补偿重构为在共享语义空间中的按需跨模态检索过程。两者在单次解码流程中统一实现。

Result: 大量实验表明，RVSD在缓解视觉幻觉方面达到最先进（SOTA）性能，并在长上下文生成场景下保持稳健的抑制能力。

Conclusion: RVSD通过训练自由的稀疏解码与语义空间视觉检索的有机结合，为缓解视觉幻觉提供了一种高效、即插即用的新范式，兼具性能优势与计算效率。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable success in vision-language tasks. However, they remain prone to Visual Hallucinations (VHs), undermining their reliability in real-world applications. Existing solutions typically require curated datasets, additional training, or multi-round decoding, resulting in considerable computational overhead. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RVSD} (\underline{R}etrieval \underline{V}ision \underline{S}parse \underline{D}ecoding), a training-free and plug-and-play decoding framework that, for the first time, unifies token sparsification and \textbf{Semantic-Space Visual Retrieval} (SSVR) within a single decoding pass. Within RVSD, we introduce a \textbf{semantics-directed token selection} strategy that selectively sparsifies redundant tokens while preserving critical visual information. We further propose the SSVR mechanism, which reformulates visual compensation as an on-demand cross-modal retrieval process within a shared semantic space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RVSD achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating VHs while maintaining robust suppression capabilities under long-context generation settings. Our code is available here.\footnote{https://github.com/canjie-liu/RVSD}

</details>


### [106] [InceptionGS: Generative Bootstrapping for Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting under Unstructured View Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02747)
*Tianheng Lu,Guangyu Wang,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InceptionGS，一种通过平衡重建与生成来解决大规模场景高斯溅射中视角稀疏（视图缺乏）问题的框架。它从初始高斯溅射出发，通过软式融入场景与视角自适应的生成先验，只修复因视图稀缺导致的问题区域，同时保留其余区域的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景数字化需要全视角一致的渲染，但由于场景复杂度、采集成本、疏忽或可达性限制，难以收集覆盖所有细节的多视角图像，导致采样视角高度非结构化——大部分场景覆盖良好但部分区域观测不足。现有基于重建的方法无法应对视图稀缺，而基于生成的方法存在泛化性、可控性和3D一致性问题。

Method: InceptionGS通过巧妙平衡重建与生成来引导高斯溅射：从初始高斯溅射出发，通过软式融入场景和视角自适应的生成先验，合理地重新审视并修复由视图稀缺引起的问题区域，同时保持其他区域的渲染质量不受影响。

Result: 在真实世界大规模场景上的大量实验表明，该方法在处理非结构化图像和提升高保真高斯溅射方面具有优越性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: InceptionGS有效解决了大规模场景中视图稀缺导致的渲染质量问题，通过重建与生成的协同平衡实现了高保真的场景重建，适用于非结构化图像采集场景。

Abstract: Achieving truly immersive large-scale scene digitization necessitates consistent and visually pleasing rendering across all possible viewing perspectives. However, collecting multi-view images covering every fine detail of a large-scale scene is prohibitive due to scene complexity, capture cost, negligence, or accessibility constraints. As a result, the sampled views tend to be highly unstructured -- the majority of the scene is well covered yet certain regions inevitably lack sufficient observations. Existing reconstruction based methods are vulnerable to view scarcity while generation based approaches suffer from generalization, controllability, and 3D consistency issues. To address this challenge, we propose InceptionGS, which bootstraps Gaussian splatting by subtly balancing reconstruction and generation. Starting from an initial Gaussian splatting, InceptionGS reasonably rethinks and repairs problematic regions caused by view scarcity while preserving the quality elsewhere, by softly incorporating scene- and view-adaptive generative priors. Extensive experiments on real-world large-scale scenes demonstrate the superiority and broad applicability of our approach in handling unstructured imagery and boosting high-fidelity Gaussian splatting. Please refer to the supplementary video for better visual demonstrations.

</details>


### [107] [Balancing Frequencies and Pixels in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02748)
*Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Arijit Ghosh,Alexei A. Efros,David Picard,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss)，一种频谱平衡损失函数，用于解决像素空间流模型中低频主导优化信号、高频细节学习缓慢的问题。通过首先强调频域学习，再过渡到像素空间v-loss的训练策略，可在无需架构改动的情况下加速收敛达40%，并持续提升FID和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像遵循1/f²频谱分布，大部分能量集中在低频，而纹理和边缘等感知关键结构占据稀疏的高频带。像素空间重建目标对所有空间误差一视同仁，导致低频主导优化信号，延缓了细粒度细节的学习。作者识别出这种目标层面的频谱失衡是训练像素空间流模型的关键低效来源。

Method: 提出Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss)频谱平衡损失，均衡各频率的学习信号，重点强化像素目标中代表性不足的高频成分。并引入简单训练策略：先强调频域学习以捕获所有频率，然后过渡到标准像素空间v-loss进行空间细化，作为流匹配损失的drop-in替代。

Result: 在多种模型规模下，所提方法加速收敛达40%，同时持续改善FID和感知保真度指标。方法无需架构修改，概念简单，作为流匹配损失的即插即用替代方案有效。

Conclusion: 频谱平衡损失结合阶段性训练策略有效缓解了像素损失的低频偏差，使训练信号与模型演进需求对齐，为像素空间流模型的训练效率提供了一种简单而有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Natural images follow a $1/f^2$ spectral distribution: most signal energy lies in the low spatial frequencies, while the perceptually important structures such as textures and edges occupy sparse high-frequency bands. Pixel-space reconstruction objectives, however, treat all spatial errors uniformly, causing low frequencies to dominate the optimization signal and delaying the learning of fine-scale details. In this work, we identify this objective-level spectral imbalance as a key inefficiency in training pixel-space flow models. To address it, we propose a Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss), a spectrally balanced objective that equalizes the learning signal across frequencies, emphasizing high-frequency components that are otherwise underrepresented in pixel-space objectives. Building on this, we introduce a simple training strategy that combines frequency and pixel supervision: we first emphasize frequency-domain learning early to capture all frequencies, and then transition to standard pixel-space v-loss for spatial refinement. This balancing mitigates the low-frequency bias of pixel losses and aligns the training signal with the evolving needs of the model. Our approach is conceptually simple, requires no architectural changes, and acts as a drop-in replacement for flow matching losses. Across multiple model scales, it accelerates convergence by up to 40% while consistently improving FID and perceptual fidelity. We will release code and models.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多工具图像编辑归属(MIEA)任务,旨在识别面部图像中涉及的多重编辑工具,并构建包含50万+图像的MultiEdit数据集,设计了DPEC方法从空间和频率域捕获局部可区分的编辑痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归属方法基于单工具假设,只能识别单一编辑工具,但实际场景中图像常经过多种工具复合编辑,痕迹相互叠加,现有方法无法处理这种复杂且日益普遍的多工具编辑场景。

Method: 1) 构建MultiEdit数据集,包含50万+编辑图像,覆盖六种工具(包括Deepfake换脸和多种面部增强);2) 提出DPEC方法,从空间域和频率域捕获可区分且局部感知的编辑工具痕迹;3) 采用基于错误的课程学习策略来支持多工具归属学习。

Result: 实验表明DPEC在最多五步编辑的面部图像上优于九种对比方法。

Conclusion: 通过定义MIEA新任务、构建大规模数据集并提出DPEC方法,本文有效解决了多工具图像编辑归属问题,验证了其在复杂编辑场景下超越现有方法的性能。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [109] [Video-Based Palm-Vein Authentication under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02776)
*Xiaofeng Yan,Kechen Liu,Abhilash Venkatesh,Cathy Zhang,Xia Zhou,Salvatore Stolfo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个公开的基于视频的掌静脉数据集CUP（哥伦比亚大学掌静脉数据集），覆盖清洁、温热、湿、脏四种表面条件并附带生理人口统计元数据。作者基准测试了21种识别器，发现脏污条件下准确率大幅下降（EER约翻两倍）。为解决此问题，提出一种无学习参数的双轴鲁棒性方案：时间上对传感器返回的少量帧取共识消除瞬时噪声，空间上使用显著性引导的区域级最优传输融合全局余弦相似度，绕开受损区域。该设计在CUP所有表面上于EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1指标领先，仅需4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs。将其附加到四个冻结的SOTA骨干网络上可降低29-37%的平均EER，且在四个公共单图像数据集上区域匹配仍有效。初步审计发现温暖条件下身体水分和性别两个群体差异通过多重比较校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景部署下掌静脉生物识别会遭遇表面噪声（汗液、污垢）、光照运动变化及温度驱动的血管可见性变化，但缺乏此类条件下采集的数据导致这些影响被低估和未充分研究。

Method: 1) 构建CUP数据集：首个公开视频级掌静脉数据集，记录四种表面条件（干净基线、温热、湿、脏）并配对生理人口统计元数据；2) 基准测试21种识别器（静态、视频、多帧聚合架构）；3) 提出双轴鲁棒性恢复：时间维度用传感器多帧共识消除瞬时污染；空间维度引入无学习参数的测试期匹配器，融合全局余弦相似度与显著性引导的区域级最优传输，绕行受损区域。

Result: 模型在干净手掌验证可靠但脏污时丢失大部分精度，平均EER约增至四倍。所提完整设计在CUP所有表面上EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1均最优，仅4.3M参数与3.1 GFLOPs（远低于视频模型成本）。附加到四个冻结SOTA骨干后均EER降低29-37%，无需重训练；四个公共单图像数据集中区域匹配独立有效。初步公平性审计发现温暖条件下身体水分与性别两个差异经受住多重比较校正。

Conclusion: CUP数据集填补了真实环境变化下掌静脉识别的数据空白，验证了无需重训练的轻量双轴（时间共识+空间最优传输）方案能大幅恢复脏污等条件下的鲁棒性，同时揭示温暖条件下跨人口统计群体存在需关注的识别差距。数据集将公开供非商业研究使用。

Abstract: Palm-vein biometrics are increasingly used for secure, contactless authentication. Yet real-world deployment exposes them to surface noise (sweat, dirt), illumination and motion variation, and temperature-driven changes in vascular visibility, which remain underexplored for lack of data captured under such conditions. To study these effects, we introduce the Columbia University Palm-vein (CUP) dataset, to our knowledge the first public video-based palm-vein dataset. CUP records every palm under four surface conditions (a clean baseline, warm, wet, and dirty) and pairs each subject with physiological and demographic metadata. On it we benchmark twenty-one recognizers spanning static, video, and multi-frame aggregation architectures. Models that verify reliably on clean palms lose most of their accuracy on dirty ones, and the mean equal error rate (EER) roughly quadruples. We recover much of that robustness along both axes of the capture. Temporally, a consensus over the few frames the sensor already returns cancels transient corruption; spatially, a test-time matcher that adds no learned parameters fuses the global cosine with a saliency-steered region-level optimal transport that routes the comparison around corrupted regions. The full design leads on every surface of CUP in EER, TAR@FAR=0.01, and Rank-1, at 4.3M parameters and 3.1 GFLOPs, a fraction of the video models' cost. Attached to four frozen state-of-the-art backbones it cuts their mean EER by 29-37% without retraining, and on four public single-image datasets the regional matching alone still helps. A preliminary audit across ten demographic and physiological traits finds two warm-condition gaps, along body water and gender, that survive multiple-comparison correction. CUP will be released for non-commercial research use at https://github.com/MobileX-CU/CUP_v1 upon publication.

</details>


### [110] [AutoCompass: Accurate Visual Localization on Public Maps by Learning from Weak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02798)
*Javier Tirado-Garín,Alan Savio Paul,Shuai Chen,Axel Barroso-Laguna,Tommaso Cavallari,Daniyar Turmukhambetov,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Eric Brachmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoCompass是一种用于训练神经地图匹配器的监督方法，它能在不依赖精确绝对姿态标签的情况下从2D地图估计图像的3自由度位姿，通过消除对航向标签的依赖、在原始GPS周围定义容差区域、以及利用SLAM或SfM获得的相对姿态来提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经地图匹配器依赖大规模地理参考图像数据集进行训练，但这些数据中的位置和航向标签常含有噪声，严重影响了训练出的模型性能。作者希望解决这一关键问题，即如何在标定不准确的情况下有效训练模型。

Method: AutoCompass包含三个关键策略：1) 从原始GPS标签训练，模型能自动学习预测准确的航向，无需显式航向标签；2) 在原始GPS周围定义容差区域来提升位置预测的准确性；3) 若有可用的SLAM或SfM结果，利用训练图像间的相对姿态作为更精确的训练信号。

Result: 在驾驶场景和以自我为中心的（egocentric）基准测试中，AutoCompass一致性地优于那些传统上强依赖绝对姿态标签训练的对照方法。

Conclusion: 该研究表明神经地图匹配器可以从不精确的绝对姿态标签中有效训练，减少了对昂贵精确标注的依赖，为实际大规模部署提供了更实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Neural map matchers estimate an image's 3-DoF pose relative to a 2D map. These models are trained on large-scale datasets of geo-referenced images, whose position and heading labels often contain noise that affects the trained models. To address this, we present AutoCompass, a supervision approach for training neural map matchers from inaccurate absolute pose labels. First, we show that heading labels are unnecessary: trained from raw GPS labels, models learn to predict accurate headings, automatically. Second, defining a tolerance region around raw GPS improves positional accuracy. Third, if available, our supervision uses relative poses between training images, obtained via SLAM or SfM, which provide a more accurate training signal. Across driving and egocentric benchmarks, AutoCompass consistently outperforms counterparts trained with the usual strong reliance on absolute pose labels.

</details>


### [111] [GDB-Reward: From Evaluation Metrics to Training Rewards for Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02813)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Purvanshi Mehta,Simon Hadfield,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GDB-Reward框架，将异构的平面设计评估指标系统地转化为统一的强化学习奖励信号，用于优化冻结的文本到图像生成模型，无需微调即可提升设计规范遵循度（排版、布局、色彩和视觉传达）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在自然图像合成上表现优异，但在平面设计任务上存在不足。设计任务的成功依赖于对排版、布局、颜色和视觉传达的精确约束。虽然提示词优化是扩散模型微调的经济替代方案，但生成过程完全不可微，需要信息丰富的奖励函数。作者提出简单问题：设计评估指标本身能否直接作为强化学习奖励？

Method: 核心是GDB-Reward框架，系统性地将异构的平面设计评估指标（涵盖感知质量、渲染保真度、空间准确性等维度）转化为统一的强化学习奖励信号。该框架利用强化学习仅需标量奖励来排序候选输出的特性，克服了生成过程不可微的障碍，同时保持图像生成器完全冻结。

Result: 实验表明GDB-Reward提供了有效的优化目标，在感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性方面大幅提升了设计规范的遵循程度，且图像生成器全程保持冻结状态。

Conclusion: 异构、不可微的评估指标可以从被动基准测试角色转变为有效的强化学习优化目标，适用于缺乏可微监督的领域。这为设计评估指标开辟了新的应用场景——作为可行动的学习信号而非仅用于事后评估。

Abstract: Text-to-image models excel at natural image synthesis but struggle with graphic design, where success depends on satisfying precise constraints on typography, layout, color, and visual communication. While prompt optimization offers an attractive alternative to expensive diffusion model fine-tuning, learning prompts for frozen image generators requires informative reward functions despite the entirely non-differentiable generation process. Reinforcement learning does not require differentiable objectives; it requires only scalar rewards capable of ranking candidate outputs. This raises a simple question: can design evaluation metrics themselves become reinforcement learning rewards? Our central contribution is GDB-Reward, a framework that systematically transforms heterogeneous graphic design evaluation metrics into a unified reinforcement learning reward. Experiments demonstrate that GDB-Reward provides an effective optimization objective, substantially improving adherence to the design specification in perceptual quality, rendering fidelity, and spatial accuracy while keeping the image generator entirely frozen. More broadly, our results demonstrate that heterogeneous, non-differentiable evaluation metrics can move beyond passive benchmarking to become effective optimization objectives for reinforcement learning in domains where differentiable supervision is unavailable.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking RAW and RGB Restoration in Image Signal Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02831)
*Zihao Lu,Radu Timofte,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于盲图像恢复的基准测试，比较了在固定ISP周围两种恢复位置（RAW域的pre-ISP恢复和sRGB域的post-ISP恢复），覆盖多种设备、ISP、退化类型和恢复模型，发现性能取决于恢复模型与成像管线的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代相机通过ISP将RAW传感器数据转换为sRGB图像，但盲图像恢复通常分别在RAW域或sRGB域进行，缺乏系统性的比较来理解放置位置对恢复性能的影响。

Method: 构建包含四个智能手机设备组、两个学习型ISP、三种退化情形的综合基准，系统比较RAW域恢复模型与sRGB域恢复模型的最佳性能。

Result: 仅改变放置位置并不能决定性能；RAW恢复优于最佳通用RGB恢复模型，但考虑了ISP变换的RGB恢复模型取得最佳整体性能；恢复性能强依赖于模型与目标成像管线的对齐。

Conclusion: 建议在报告中明确恢复放置位置和ISP感知监督作为关键实验因素，为图像恢复研究提供更合理的评估框架。

Abstract: Modern cameras transform RAW sensor measurements into sRGB images through an image signal processor (ISP). We benchmark two placements for blind restoration around a fixed ISP: (A) pre-ISP restoration in the RAW domain and (B) post-ISP restoration in the sRGB domain. The benchmark covers four smartphone device groups, two learned ISPs, three degradation regimes--noise, blur, and joint noise and blur--, and several representative RAW and RGB restoration models. Our results show that placement alone does not determine performance. The RAW restoration strategy outperforms the best generic RGB restoration models. However, RGB restoration models trained considering the ISP transformations, achieve the best overall performance. Our novel benchmark demonstrates that the image reconstruction performance strongly depends on the alignment between the restoration model and the target imaging pipeline. We consequently recommend reporting restoration placement and ISP-aware supervision as key experimental factors. Our code is available at https://github.com/mv-lab/AISP

</details>


### [113] [Efficient All-in-One Weather Restoration using Spectral Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02839)
*Paula Garrido-Mellado,Daniel Feijoo,Yuning Cui,Alvaro Garcia,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FReSH-IR，一种轻量级全合一图像复原方法，通过在分层编码器-解码器架构的每个尺度上显式分解高低频成分，并结合基于傅里叶的跳跃连接实现频率重构与频谱调和，在保持与transformer模型相近复原质量的同时减少80%的参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气（雨、雾、雪）会显著降低图像质量，影响人类感知和物理AI。现有复原方法计算开销大，难以处理高分辨率图像和多种退化类型，亟需轻量级且能处理各种降质的解决方案。

Method: 提出频率重构通过频谱调和（Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization），在分层编码器-解码器架构的每个尺度上显式将特征表示分解为高低频分量；通过傅里叶域跳跃连接将频谱分解与空间处理相结合，实现互补频率信息捕获而不损失空间细节。

Result: 与基于transformer的模型相比，在达到相似复原质量的前提下减少了80%的参数和运算量。大量实验证明了该方法具有优异的效率-性能平衡，适用于资源受限系统。

Conclusion: FReSH-IR展示了显著的效率-性能权衡优势，体现了其在资源受限系统中的实际应用价值，为恶劣天气下的高效全合一图像复原提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Adverse weather conditions such as rain, haze, and snow significantly degrade image quality, posing challenges for both human perception and physical AI. Existing restoration methods require large computational budgets, struggling to process high-resolution images and handle different degradations. In this paper, we present Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization, a novel lightweight all-in-one restoration method that explicitly decomposes feature representations into high- and low-frequency components at each scale of a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture. By combining spectral decomposition with spatial processing through Fourier-based skip connections, FReSH-IR captures complementary frequency information without sacrificing spatial detail. Our approach achieves similar restoration quality with 80% fewer parameters and operations than transformer-based models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method offers a great efficiency-performance trade-off, highlighting its practical applications in constrained-resource systems.

</details>


### [114] [RoGe: Novel View Synthesis via End-to-End Implicit Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02847)
*Xiaolei Lang,Ze Kang,Zehao Huang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoGe是一个端到端的统一重建与生成框架，用于稀疏视图下的新视角合成（漫游视频生成）。它通过前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表征，并用目标相机光线查询得到逐视图几何特征，直接注入视频扩散模型作为条件，无需显式3D中间表示。两模块联合训练，生成目标直接塑造其几何条件。在DL3DV数据集上优于重建、生成及混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合方法（融合重建与生成）通过渲染图像或显式3D表示（如点图、3D高斯）作为桥梁将两者连接，但这种方式存在局限：生成的输入是有损且不完美的场景投影，会继承其误差；同时重建模块无法从生成模块的信号中修正这些误差。

Method: RoGe移除显式3D桥梁：1) 用稀疏输入视图通过前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表征；2) 以目标相机光线查询该表征，获得逐视图几何特征；3) 将这些特征直接注入视频扩散模型作为条件；4) 两个模块联合训练，使生成损失直接作用于几何条件的学习。

Result: 在DL3DV数据集上，RoGe在图像级指标和视频级时间一致性上均优于基于重建、生成及混合方法的基线。消融实验表明：光线查询的隐式特征优于原始重建token和渲染RGB作为条件；联合训练带来进一步性能提升。

Conclusion: 去除显式3D中间表示、将几何条件以光线查询隐式特征直接注入生成模型，并采用联合训练，是提升稀疏视图漫游视频合成质量的有效途径。该方法证明了重建与生成可以在无损失性中间投影的情况下深度融合。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs requires both geometric grounding from the observed views and generative priors of unobserved regions, motivating recent hybrid methods that combine reconstruction and generation. However, existing methods bridge the two with rendered images or explicit 3D representations such as point maps or 3D Gaussians. Generation is thus conditioned on a lossy and imperfect projection of the scene, inheriting its errors, and reconstruction receives no signal from generation to correct them. We present RoGe, an end-to-end unified reconstruction and generation framework that removes this explicit bridge. It targets roaming within a scene anchored by sparse views: given a few posed images and a camera trajectory, it synthesizes a temporally coherent video along that trajectory. From the sparse input views, RoGe builds an implicit scene representation with a feed-forward reconstruction model, and queries it with target camera rays to obtain per-view geometric features. These features are injected into a video diffusion model as conditioning, without any 3D intermediate. Both modules are trained jointly, so the generation objective directly shapes its own geometric conditioning. We conduct experiments on DL3DV, where RoGe outperforms reconstruction-based, generation-based, and hybrid baselines on image-level metrics and video-level temporal consistency. Ablations confirm that ray-queried implicit features outperform both raw reconstruction tokens and rendered RGB as conditioning, and that joint training brings further gains.

</details>


### [115] [MuyBridge: Mobile Human Center-of-Mass Estimation from Monocular Video via Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02854)
*Aidan Bradshaw,Marco Giordano,David Rode,Andreas Habersack,Elif Basokur,Annika Kruse,Markus Tilp,Michele Magno,Peter Wolf,Luca Benini,Christoph Leitner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuyBridge，一个在单摄像头手机上实时估计运动员三维质心（CoM）轨迹的端侧系统。它通过紧凑的2D姿态网络与蒸馏的单步单目深度网络结合，借助解剖与物理先验进行解析度量融合，无需3D或任务特定监督，即可在iPhone上实现高精度CoM估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维质心（CoM）是运动、康复和临床生物力学分析中的核心量，但现有方法要么仅优化3D关键点精度而缺乏解剖约束，要么依赖过重的计算与采集基础设施，难以部署到运动员训练和比赛的真实场景。因此，在单相机条件下获得度量级别的CoM仍然困难。

Method: MuyBridge将紧凑型2D姿态网络与蒸馏后的单步单目深度网络相结合，通过解析度量融合，利用解剖与物理先验来锚定度量CoM。该方法不需要任何3D标注或任务特定监督，并采用一次性标定。

Result: 在AthletePose3D数据集（跑步、田径、花样滑冰）上，垂直CoM误差为33-41毫米，绝对相对范围误差（AbsRel）为2.3-6.6%。在iPhone 15上以63 FPS的姿态估计速率输出CoM，深度更新为异步2.86 Hz。

Conclusion: MuyBridge证明了在普通手机上即可实现高精度、实时的运动员质心轨迹估计，填补了从单相机现场测量度量CoM的空白，可为教练和运动分析师提供易部署的实用工具。

Abstract: The 3D center of mass (CoM) is a primary quantity in the biomechanical analysis of sport, rehabilitation, and clinical movement, yet existing 3D pose tracking, mesh recovery, and multi-view triangulation methods either optimize 3D keypoint accuracy without anatomical constraints or carry compute and capture infrastructure too heavy to deploy where CoM tracking is most useful. As a result, the metric CoM remains difficult for coaches and movement analysts to measure from a single camera where athletes train and compete. In this work, we introduce MuyBridge, an on-device system that estimates the athlete's segmental center of mass trajectory from a single phone camera video stream. MuyBridge couples a compact 2D pose network and a distilled single-step monocular depth network through an analytic metric fusion that uses anatomical and physical priors to anchor the metric CoM, requiring no 3D or task-specific supervision. Evaluated on the athletic movements of AthletePose3D (running, track and field, and figure skating), MuyBridge achieves 33-41 mm vertical CoM error and 2.3-6.6% absolute-relative range error (AbsRel) under a one-time calibration, and produces CoM estimates at the 63 FPS pose-estimation rate using asynchronous 2.86 Hz depth updates on iPhone 15. Code is available at: https://github.com/Abradshaw1/Muybridge

</details>


### [116] [PlantC2USeg: Cross-Scale Consistent Pre-Training for Few-Shot Unified Plant Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02860)
*Yu Tian,Xintong Jiang,Jan Franklin Adamowski,Shiv O. Prasher,Shangpeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlantC2USeg 是一个面向植物点云分割的深度迁移学习框架，通过跨尺度一致性学习和信息受限解码策略，实现少样本跨物种、跨传感条件的鲁棒泛化，在多种数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物育种需要精确的器官级分析，植物点云分割（PPCS）日益重要。然而传统深度学习方法依赖大量密集标注数据，获取成本高昂；且面对分布偏移（不同物种、传感条件）的统一PPCS适应仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出PlantC2USeg框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 跨尺度一致性学习（cross-scale consistency learning），显式对齐不同空间尺度的特征；(2) 信息受限解码策略（information-restricted decoding strategy），防止重建捷径并促进鲁棒适应。预训练支持少样本跨物种泛化，统一微调配合继承阈值降低适应开销。

Result: 全监督下Soybean3D上语义IoU达91.91%、实例mWCov达94.62%；20样本下两项指标分别为89.78%和90.27%；10样本下mWCov保持83.23%、IoU为83.19%。跨HR3D数据集10-shot迁移至烟草、番茄和高粱平均IoU 78.41%、mWCov 79.42%；22-shot迁移至SYAU-Maize实现IoU 92.75%、mRec 93.51%。ShapeNet Part上类别平均mIoU达85.0%。

Conclusion: PlantC2USeg在分布偏移下减少整体适应成本，支持可扩展的植物表型分析以及超越农业领域的可迁移3D表示学习。

Abstract: Modern crop breeding demands precise organ-level analysis for trait quantification, making plant point cloud segmentation (PPCS) increasingly important. However, conventional deep learning approaches rely heavily on densely annotated datasets that are labor-intensive to acquire. Unified PPCS adaptation from distribution-shifted examples with minimal additional training remains challenging. To address this, we propose PlantC2USeg, a deep transfer learning framework featuring cross-scale consistency learning to explicitly align features across spatial scales and an information-restricted decoding strategy that prevents reconstruction shortcuts and promotes robust adaptation. The resulting pre-training enables stable few-shot generalization across species and sensing conditions, while unified fine-tuning with inherited thresholds further reduces adaptation overhead. Under full supervision on Soybean3D, PlantC2USeg achieves the highest semantic IoU and instance mWCov among compared methods, at 91.91% and 94.62%. With 20 labeled samples, it leads both metrics at 89.78% and 90.27%; with only 10 samples, it retains the highest mWCov of 83.23% while achieving 83.19% IoU. Across HR3D, 10-shot transfer to tobacco, tomato, and sorghum averages 78.41% IoU and 79.42% mWCov, while 22-shot transfer to SYAU-Maize achieves the highest IoU and mRec at 92.75% and 93.51%. Furthermore, a leading category-averaged mIoU of 85.0% on ShapeNet Part demonstrates the framework's capability to handle diverse shape variations beyond agricultural domains. These results demonstrate that PlantC2USeg reduces overall adaptation effort under distribution shifts, enabling scalable plant phenotyping and transferable 3D representation learning beyond agriculture.

</details>


### [117] [Thinking in Pictures: A Systematic Benchmark for Reasoning-driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02864)
*Yutong Liu,Nan Huang,Xu Cao,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍RIG-BENCH，一个系统性评估"推理驱动图像生成"(RIG)能力的综合性基准，涵盖概念、变换、模式结构与场景四大认知维度，共2000个样本。对现有统一生成模型(UCMs)和图像/视频生成模型的大量评估揭示了显著的"推理-生成鸿沟"：模型常产生局部合理但全局不合逻辑的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管统一生成模型和世界模拟器在视觉感知与合成上取得了突破，但它们主要依赖表层的"事件对齐"，缺乏从视觉输入推断潜在规则并通过精确、逻辑约束的视觉结果来展现解决方案的高层推理能力（即"推理-生成"能力），这一方向尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建RIG-BENCH基准，从四个认知要求高的领域系统评估RIG能力：(1)概念驱动、(2)变换驱动、(3)模式与结构、(4)场景驱动，包含2000个精心策划的样本作为严格压力测试。

Result: 对最先进UCMs及图像/视频生成模型的大量评估揭示了显著的推理-生成差距——模型经常生成局部看似合理但全局不合逻辑的输出。

Conclusion: RIG-BENCH为开发下一代逻辑自洽的统一生成模型和世界模拟器提供了关键的诊断框架。

Abstract: Recent advancements in unified generative models (UGMs) and world simulators have achieved unprecedented results in visual perception and synthesis. However, these models primarily rely on surface-level event alignment, leaving the capacity for high-level visual reasoning underexplored. True visual generative intelligence demands "Reasoning-to-Generation", an ability to infer latent rules from visual inputs and manifest solutions through precise, logically constrained visual outcomes. We introduce RIG-BENCH, a novel comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates Reasoning-driven Image Generation (RIG) across four cognitively demanding domains: Concept-based, Transformation-based, Pattern & Structure, and Scenario-based. Featuring 2000 curated samples, RIG-BENCH serves as a rigorous stress test for RIG. Our extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art UGMs and image/video generation models reveal a significant reasoning-generation gap, wherein models frequently produce locally plausible but globally illogical outputs. RIG-BENCH provides a vital diagnostic framework to guide the development of next-generation, logically grounded UGMs and world simulators.

</details>


### [118] [SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02886)
*Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Xianghao Kong,Zhuoran Zhao,Wei Huang,Yihua Du,Zixin Zhang,Justin Cui,Yuchao Gu,Yukang Chen,Xinting Hu,Tianyu He,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Xin Wang,Mike Zheng Shou,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarWM是一个完全开源的交互式视频世界模型基础框架，从数据准备到长时程推理全流程覆盖。它通过可重构的多源数据引擎（将10个数据集、143万条片段统一为帧对齐的数据契约）和骨干网络原生适配框架，在共享接口下实例化了四个基于Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3的5B-33B模型，并通过三阶段训练策略（双向适配、教师强制的自回归初始化、分布匹配蒸馏）实现仅用5秒序列训练即可支持分钟到小时级别的实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建交互式视频世界模型面临两大挑战：异构数据源在时间尺度、相机几何、视觉质量、运动和标题风格上差异巨大；不同视频生成器使用不同的表示和架构。朴素的数据混合和模型特定实现会导致监督不一致，且难以复现和比较。

Method: SolarWM提出两个核心组件：(1)可重构多源数据引擎，将143万条片段从10个数据集转换为统一的帧对齐契约（涵盖视觉观测、度量相机几何、标题、质量元数据、选择决策和来源），解耦源处理与混合构建；(2)骨干网络原生适配框架，在共享的相机条件化、训练和推理接口下，保留Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3的原生表示和优化目标。训练采用统一的三阶段流程：双向适配、教师强制的自回归初始化、分布匹配蒸馏。

Result: 成功实例化四个5B-33B的自回归因果模型，仅用5秒序列训练即可实现分钟到小时级别的实时交互，并开源了全部数据、流水线、配方、权重和框架。

Conclusion: SolarWM通过统一数据契约和骨干原生适配，解决了异构世界模型训练中的一致性和复现性问题，为交互式世界模型研究提供了可复现、可扩展的开放基础。

Abstract: We introduce SolarWM, a fully open foundation for building interactive video world models from data preparation through long-horizon inference. Training across heterogeneous data sources and video backbones is challenging: datasets differ in temporal scale, camera geometry, visual quality, motion, and captioning styles, while video generators use distinct representations and architectures. Naive data mixing and model-specific implementations therefore produce inconsistent supervision and make results difficult to reproduce and compare. SolarWM addresses this coupling with a reconfigurable multi-source data engine and a backbone-native adaptation framework. The engine converts 1.43 million canonical clips from 10 datasets into a unified, frame-aligned contract covering visual observations, metric camera geometry, captions, quality metadata, selection decisions, and provenance, while decoupling source processing from mixture construction. Under shared camera-conditioning, training, and inference interfaces, we instantiate four 5B--33B models based on Wan2.2, LTX-2.5, and MiniMax-H3 while preserving their native representations and objectives. A unified three-stage recipe combines bidirectional adaptation, teacher-forced autoregressive initialization, and distribution matching distillation. The resulting causal models enable real-time interaction over rollouts ranging from minutes to hours after being trained on only 5s sequences. By releasing the resulting data, pipeline, recipes, weights, and framework, SolarWM provides a reproducible and extensible foundation for interactive world-model research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [PRO-Step: Step-level Process Reward Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01658)
*MinKeon Kim,Namjun Lee,Jaekwang Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRO-STEP通过训练生成式过程奖励模型(PRM)来同时评估RAG中每个推理步骤的逻辑有效性（logical validity）和证据支撑（evidential grounding），并采用PRM引导的价值树搜索构建对比偏好对，配合步骤级直接偏好优化(step-level DPO)训练策略。在五个单跳和多跳问答基准上取得了最佳的平均EM和F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中的多跳推理容易因早期检索失败导致错误传播；标准的基于结果的优化仅奖励最终答案，无法检测中间检索和推理错误；现有基于过程的奖励方法仍然以最终答案作为步骤评分基准，会奖励那些检索有缺陷但碰巧得出正确答案的"虚假成功"步骤。

Method: 提出PRO-STEP框架：(1) 训练一个既能评估逻辑有效性又能评估证据支撑的生成式过程奖励模型(PRM)；(2) 利用PRM引导的价值树搜索来构建偏好对，对比有效步骤与缺陷步骤；(3) 通过步骤级直接偏好优化(step-level DPO)优化策略。

Result: 在五个单跳和多跳QA基准数据集上，PRO-STEP取得了最佳的平均EM(Exact Match)和F1分数，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: PRO-STEP证明了在RAG场景中，步骤级监督需要同时考虑逻辑有效性和证据支撑两个维度，通过生成式PRM结合价值树搜索和步骤级DPO，可以有效减少错误传播并提升多跳推理性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation enhances Large Language Models by grounding responses in external knowledge, but multi-hop reasoning remains vulnerable to error propagation, where early retrieval failures confound subsequent steps. Standard outcome-based optimization only rewards the final answer, leaving intermediate retrieval and reasoning errors undetected. While existing process-based methods introduce step-level signals, they still score each step against the final answer, rewarding spurious successes where flawed retrieval coincidentally produces the correct answer. Step-level supervision in RAG requires evaluating both logical validity and evidential grounding at each step. We introduce PRO-STEP: we train a generative PRM that evaluates both dimensions, employ PRM-guided value tree search to construct preference pairs contrasting valid steps against flawed ones, and optimize the policy via step-level Direct Preference Optimization. Experiments on single and multi-hop QA datasets demonstrate that PRO-STEP achieves the best average EM and F1 across five benchmarks. Code, models, and training data are publicly available at https://github.com/keemminnke/PRO-Step.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Evidence Sufficiency Boundaries for Selective Answering in Grounded Multi-Hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01687)
*Haruto Sato,Yuki Tanaka,Ren Nakamura,Aoi Kobayashi,Mei Ito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出证据充分性边界训练（Evidence Sufficiency Boundary Training, ESBT），一种面向生成模型的训练框架，用于多跳问答中的选择性回答。该方法通过构建有序证据链，直接监督模型从"弃答"到"作答"的转换边界，在证据不足时弃答、证据充分时作答、冗余证据加入时保持答案稳定。实验表明该方法在边界定位精度和外部不可答数据集上的不支持回答率方面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有依据的问答系统中，模型应仅在证据支持答案时才作答。多跳QA中，部分证据会使不支持的回答看似合理，因此模型需要识别"证据何时足够"这一关键边界——在证据不充分时弃答，首次充分时作答，冗余证据加入时答案保持稳定。

Method: 提出证据充分性边界训练框架，核心组件包括：(1) 水平监督（level supervision），从有序证据链中标注各证据水平的状态；(2) 边界翻转边际（boundary flip margin），强化弃答到作答的转换；(3) 边界后稳定性（post-boundary stability），保证冗余证据下答案不变；(4) 答案召回保护（answer recall protection）。利用HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue构建证据链，在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上使用LoRA适配训练。

Result: 在最强边界定位上，翻转准确率达0.807，优于token级弃答基线的0.781；在外部不可答评估集上达到了最低的整体不支持回答率0.095（对比基线0.101），同时保持有竞争力的原始QA F1。

Conclusion: 有依据的选择性回答性能在训练显式标记"拒绝应让位于作答"的证据水平时获得改进，说明直接监督证据充分性边界是提升多跳QA选择性回答能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Grounded question answering systems should answer only when the supplied evidence supports the answer. In multi-hop QA, this requirement is difficult because partial evidence can make an unsupported answer appear plausible. We study selective answering through evidence sufficiency boundaries: for the same question, a model should abstain under unsupported or partially supported context, answer when the context first becomes sufficient, and keep the answer stable when redundant evidence is added. We introduce Evidence Sufficiency Boundary Training, a generation-native training framework that constructs ordered evidence chains and supervises the abstain-to-answer transition directly. The method combines level supervision, a boundary flip margin, post-boundary stability, and answer recall protection. We build evidence chains from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, then evaluate models with chain metrics, raw QA utility, and unsupported-answer rates on external non-answerable sets. With Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and LoRA adaptation, Evidence Sufficiency Boundary Training gives the strongest boundary localization among the tested systems, with flip accuracy of 0.807 compared with 0.781 for a token-level abstention baseline. It also achieves the lowest overall unsupported-answer rate on external non-answerable evaluation, 0.095 compared with 0.101 for the same baseline, while retaining competitive raw QA F1. The results show that grounded selective answering improves when training marks the evidence level where refusal should give way to answering.

</details>


### [121] [SpeakPay: Domain-Adaptive LoRA Fine-Tuning of Whisper for Low-Resource Nepali Financial Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01737)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpeakPay，一个面向尼泊尔视障用户的语音优先数字钱包，核心贡献是低资源金融语音识别的领域自适应受控研究。研究引入NepFinSpeech-403数据集（403条尼泊尔金融语音命令），使用LoRA微调Whisper large-v2，将词错误率从129.95%降至42.58%（相对降低67.2%），交易成功率从1.67%提升至33.33%（约20倍增益），并发布公开可访问的语音优先网页应用及全部代码、数据和模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔的移动支付应用以图形界面为主，对视障用户极不友好。现有语音识别系统在低资源尼泊尔金融语音场景下表现极差（零样本WER高达129.95%），亟需针对该领域的语音识别自适应研究，以提升金融交易的可及性与成功率。

Method: 构建NepFinSpeech-403数据集（403条覆盖send、load、balance三种操作及237个独特数字的尼泊尔金融语音命令），采用LoRA对Whisper large-v2进行领域微调，并进行数据效率分析、错误分析和逐话语级别的显著性检验（符号检验）评估。

Result: 领域自适应模型在测试集上将WER从129.95%降至42.58%（相对降低67.2%），天城文数字识别准确率从0.0%提升至73.9%；交易成功率从1.67%提升至33.33%（约20倍提升），跨命令类型一致改善（符号检验p<10^-17）。仅需100条领域数据即可将零样本WER减半，约300条达到性能平台期。错误分析揭示数字混淆模式（零插入/删除、前缀幻觉）是剩余交易失败的主因。

Conclusion: 领域自适应对低资源金融语音识别具有显著且统计上稳健的收益；词级指标低估了实际任务级影响，应以交易成功率为核心评估指标；少量领域数据即可带来大幅改进，但剩余错误主要源于系统性的数字混淆模式，仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: Mobile payment applications in Nepal are graphically mediated and largely inaccessible to visually impaired users. This paper presents SpeakPay, a voice-first digital wallet, and documents the central technical contribution: a controlled study of domain adaptation for low-resource financial speech recognition. We introduce NepFinSpeech-403, a 403-utterance dataset of Nepali financial voice commands (send, load, and balance operations spanning 237 unique numerals), and fine-tune Whisper large-v2 with LoRA. On the held-out test set, the domain-adapted model reduces Word Error Rate from 129.95% (zero-shot baseline) to 42.58% --- a 67.2% relative reduction --- and improves Devanagari numeral recognition accuracy from 0.0% to 73.9%. We find that word-level metrics understate the practical task-level impact: domain adaptation improves the Transaction Success Rate from 1.67% to 33.33%, a roughly 20x gain. The improvement is consistent at the individual-utterance level (sign test, $p < 10^{-17}$) and across all command types. A data efficiency analysis shows that as few as 100 domain-specific utterances are sufficient to halve the zero-shot WER, with performance plateauing around 300 examples. Error analysis reveals systematic numeral confusion patterns (zero insertion/deletion, prefix hallucination) that account for the majority of remaining transaction failures. The trained system is deployed as a publicly accessible voice-first web application. All code, dataset, model weights, and this paper are released at https://github.com/subedibiraj/speakpay.

</details>


### [122] [MemeCULT-1K: Benchmarking South Asian Cultural Context and Humor Understanding of Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01772)
*Tawsif Tashwar Dipto,Mehedi Ahamed,Radib Bin Kabir,Mueeze Al Mushabbir,Mohammed Saidul Islam,Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemeCULT-1K，一个包含1,000个南亚表情包（孟加拉语、英语、印地语）的多语言基准数据集，每个表情包配有文化背景注释和三个人工撰写的解释，另有54个孟加拉地区方言表情包作为补充。作者评估了13个流行VLM在"仅表情包"和"上下文感知"两种设置下的表现，发现提供最小文化背景在所有模型和语言上均带来一致改进（SBERT相似度提升+11.8，BLEURT提升+5.0，LLM评判分数提升+0.86）。细粒度错误分析显示，闭源模型主要失败于实体和指代误识别，开源模型受限于更广泛的文化知识缺口，而语言和音韵学失败对两者都最具上下文抵抗力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情包理解超越了视觉内容或字面文字的识别，需要隐含的文化知识和语用推理，而大多数视觉语言模型仍缺乏这种能力。现有研究缺少专门针对文化敏感表情包理解的多语言基准，特别是南亚地区（孟加拉语、英语、印地语）的表情包数据集，这阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建MemeCULT-1K多语言基准数据集：包含1,000个南亚表情包配以文化上下文注释和三个人工解释，外加54个方言补充集；在两种设置（仅表情包与文化背景感知）下评估13个流行VLM；使用SBERT相似度、BLEURT和LLM-as-a-Judge等多维度评分指标；并进行细粒度错误分析以分类模型失败模式。

Result: 提供最小文化背景在全部模型和语言上带来一致性能提升：SBERT相似度从44.6提升至56.4（+11.8），BLEURT从37.3提升至42.3（+5.0），LLM评判分数从2.57提升至3.43（+0.86，满分5分）。闭源模型主要失败于实体与指代误识别；开源模型的瓶颈在于更广泛的文化知识缺口；语言和音韵学失败对两类型模型都最具上下文抵抗力（即最难通过背景信息改善）。

Conclusion: 实验结果凸显了具有文化根基的表情包理解的困难性，仅靠最小文化背景就能带来显著提升说明显式文化知识整合是未来工作的关键方向。数据集和代码已公开，供社区使用和进一步研究。

Abstract: Meme understanding goes beyond recognizing visual content or literal text; it requires implicit cultural knowledge and pragmatic inference that most vision-language models still lack. We introduce MemeCULT-1K, a multilingual benchmark of 1,000 South Asian memes in Bengali, English, and Hindi, where each meme is paired with a cultural context note and three human-written explanations, along with a supplementary set of 54 Bengali regional dialect memes. We evaluate thirteen popular Vision Language Models (VLMs) under two settings: meme-only and context-aware. Providing minimal cultural context yields consistent gains across all models and languages: mean SBERT similarity improves from 44.6 to 56.4 (+11.8), BLEURT from 37.3 to 42.3 (+5.0), and LLM-as-a-Judge scores from 2.57 to 3.43 out of 5 (+0.86). Fine-grained error analysis reveals that closed-source models fail mainly on entity and reference misidentification, while open-source models are bottlenecked by broader cultural knowledge gaps, with linguistic and phonological failures proving the most context-resistant across both. These results highlight the difficulty of culturally grounded meme understanding and motivate future work on explicit cultural knowledge integration. Our dataset and code are publicly available at TawsifDipto17/MemeCULT-1K.

</details>


### [123] [VakyArth: Evaluating Pragmatic Competence in LLMs across Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01788)
*Usneek Singh,Poorvaja Veera Balaji Kumar,Parth Nanda,Anand Madhusoodanan,Geyang Guo,Wei Xu,Junyi Jessy L*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VakyArth，这是首个针对印度语言的语用学基准测试，涵盖印地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语和马拉雅拉姆语，评估五种语用现象。研究发现多语言大模型在这些语言的语用理解上存在系统性失败，且不同语言和任务间表现存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语用学评估主要集中在英语和高资源语言上，缺乏对印度语言（Indic languages）的探索。尽管这些语言具有丰富的语言和文化多样性，其语用推理能力尚未得到充分评估，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建VakyArth基准测试，覆盖印地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语和马拉雅拉姆语四种印度语言，评估五种语用现象：指示语（deixis）、言语行为（speech acts）、隐含意义（implicature）、社会语用（social pragmatics）和连贯性（coherence）。采用多项选择题、自然语言推断和翻译三种任务形式，所有题目由母语者撰写。

Result: 多种多语言大模型在基于印度语言和文化惯例的语用意义上持续失败。任务间存在系统性差异：所有模型-语言组合中，多项选择题准确率高于自然语言推断准确率；翻译表现不能可靠反映语用理解水平；印度-雅利安语系语言在翻译上优于达罗毗荼语系语言。此外，自动翻译指标可能遗漏流畅但语用不忠实的输出，尤其体现在隐含意义和指示语方面。

Conclusion: 现有主流多语言大模型在印度语言的语用推理上存在显著不足，任务形式、语系类别和现象类型均会影响评估结果，表明需要专门针对语用理解的评估方法和模型改进方向。

Abstract: Real-world communication often requires pragmatic reasoning: interpreting meanings implied through context and cultural convention rather than stated literally. Existing pragmatic evaluation remains largely limited to English and high-resource languages, leaving Indic languages unexplored despite their linguistic and cultural diversity. We introduce VakyArth, the first pragmatic benchmark for Indic languages, designed as a diagnostic evaluation covering Hindi, Punjabi, Tamil, and Malayalam. VakyArth evaluates models across five phenomena: deixis, speech acts, implicature, social pragmatics, and coherence; through multiple-choice questions, natural language inference, and translation, with all items authored by native speakers. Across multilingual large language models (LLMs) of varying families and sizes, we find consistent failures on pragmatic meanings rooted in Indic linguistic and cultural conventions. Our analysis shows systematic differences across languages and tasks: MCQ accuracy exceeds NLI accuracy in all model-language combinations, translation performance does not reliably track pragmatic understanding, and Indo-Aryan languages show a translation advantage over Dravidian languages. We further show that automatic translation metrics can miss fluent but pragmatically unfaithful outputs, especially for implicature and deixis.

</details>


### [124] [Disentangling Statistical Preemption from Entrenchment in Language Models' Avoidance of Overgeneralization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01794)
*Yixuan Wang,Freda Shi,Kanishka Misra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在儿童-看护者对话语料上训练语言模型（LM），系统性地移除抢占性（preemptive）与非抢占性证据，来检验语言学习者如何在没有显性负面证据的情况下避免过度泛化（如"Tom laughed me"）。研究发现LM虽能避免过度泛化，但在动词特定层面未表现出抢占效应，仅呈现微弱的抽象抢占证据，且LM将竞争性结构视为间接正面而非负面证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言学习者如何在没有显性负面证据的情况下避免过度泛化（如Tom laughed me）是语言习得中的核心难题。构式语法提出了两种间接负面证据机制——抢占（preemption）和固化（entrenchment），但以往研究难以在人类学习者中分离这两种假说，需要通过可控实验来厘清其作用机制。

Method: 采用受控饲养实验（controlled rearing experiments）方法，在儿童-看护者对话语料上训练语言模型，系统性地移除抢占性证据（如she made him laugh这类近义构式）与非抢占性证据（如he laughed这类普通用法），以分离两种假说，并结合训练动态分析来考察LM的学习机制。

Result: LM确实能避免过度泛化，但在动词特定层面未表现出抢占机制；仅在抽象层面检测到微弱但非零的抢占证据。训练动态分析表明，在动词特定条件下，LM将竞争性结构视为间接正面证据而非间接负面证据。

Conclusion: 如果抢占是比固化更合理的人类避免过度泛化的途径，那么本研究结果说明神经网络的机器学习者需要具备对间接负面证据的敏感性。研究同时为检验人类抽象抢占效应提供了新实验设计的启示。

Abstract: How do learners avoid overgeneralizations such as Tom laughed me without explicit negative evidence? Constructionists have posited two proposals that describe indirect negative evidence against overgeneralizations: preemption (which privileges exposure to near-synonymous construction---e.g., she made him laugh) vs. entrenchment (all exposures to a verb's grammatical usages, including cases like He laughed). We disentangle these hypotheses by running controlled rearing experiments on LMs trained on child-caregiver conversations, where we systematically remove preemptive vs. non-preemptive evidence. We find that while LMs avoid overgeneralizations, they do not show preemption at a verb-specific level, instead showing weak but non-zero evidence of abstract preemption. Combined with results from analyzing the LMs' training dynamics, we find that LMs treat competing structures as indirect positive---as opposed to negative---evidence in the verb-specific condition. Insofar as preemption is the more plausible route to avoiding overgeneralizations in humans, our results point the need for there to be sensitivities to indirect negative evidence in neural network learners, and suggest new human experiments to test abstract preemption.

</details>


### [125] [How Do Prompt Variations Affect Energy Consumption in On-Device LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01798)
*Wei Hu,Xiaolong Tu,Dawei Chen,Yitao Chen,Kyungtae Han,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示词设计（认知负荷和措辞模式）对移动端LLM推理能耗的影响，通过跨数据集、模型和设备的实证研究，发现认知负荷主要影响每token能耗，而措辞模式主要通过token使用量影响能耗，并揭示了提示词设计对能耗-质量前沿的影响因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地部署在移动设备上，能效成为关键部署约束，但提示词设计对能耗的影响尚未得到充分探索。理解提示词属性如何影响能耗对于实现能效优化的端侧LLM推理至关重要。

Method: 作者进行了广泛的实证研究，覆盖提示词属性、数据集、模型和设备多个维度，采用阶段级（phase-level）性能剖析，将推理过程划分为prefill（预填充）和decode（解码）两个阶段分别测量能耗。

Result: 研究发现认知负荷主要影响每token的能耗成本，而措辞模式主要通过影响token使用量来影响总体能耗。能耗-质量分析进一步表明提示词设计重塑可达到的前沿（frontier）的方式因模型而异。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在能效优化的端侧LLM推理中需要采用模型感知（model-aware）的提示词设计策略，因为不同模型对提示词设计变化的能耗响应存在差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, making energy efficiency a key deployment constraint, yet the energy impact of prompt design remains underexplored. This paper aims to understand how two prompt properties, cognitive load and phrasing pattern, shape the energy behavior of on-device LLM inference. We conduct a broad empirical study covering prompt properties, datasets, models, and devices, with phase-level profiling that separates prefill and decode energy. We find that cognitive load primarily affects the energy cost per token, while phrasing pattern affects energy largely through token usage. Our energy-quality analysis further shows that prompt design reshapes the attainable frontier differently across models, highlighting the need for model-aware prompt design in energy-efficient on-device LLM inference. Code, datasets, and scripts are available at https://amai-gsu.github.io/PromptProperty/.

</details>


### [126] [TalkFa: A Unified Benchmark for Farsi Dialogue Generation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01810)
*Neda Jamshidi,Kamyar Zeinalipour,Fahimeh Akbari,Monica Bianchini,Marco Maggini,Marco Gori*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TALKFA，一个针对波斯语的首个综合对话基准，包含三个互补数据集（知识型对话、对话行为与情感标注、戏剧对话情感标注），并进行了广泛的模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语拥有超过1.2亿使用者，但缺乏全面的对话生成与理解基准。现有资源无法支持该语言对话系统的评估与发展，需要建立统一的评测基准。

Method: 构建三个互补数据集：WIKI-FADIAL（4.2K维基百科知识型对话）、DAILYDIALOG-FA（6.6K对话带有对话行为和情感标注）、PLAYDIAL-FA（2.1K戏剧对话带情感标签）。利用LLM辅助数据构建，但所有对话经过母语者的多阶段审查修订，仅发布经人工批准的数据。评估涉及六种LLAMA和MISTRAL模型，涵盖LoRA微调、GPT-4.1作为评判者等。

Result: LoRA仅需25-50%的训练数据即可恢复90%以上的性能增益；FABERT在对话行为任务上最佳，LORA-MISTRAL-7B在情感识别上最佳，MISTRAL-24B在情感评分上最高；自动指标显著高估对话质量；零样本评估显示TALKFA对前沿LLM仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: TALKFA是可靠且具有挑战性的波斯语对话基准，人类评估和外部验证证实了其可靠性，且将公开所有资源以促进波斯语对话系统的研究。

Abstract: Farsi, spoken by more than 120 million people, lacks a comprehensive benchmark for dialogue generation and understanding. We introduce TALKFA, a unified benchmark comprising three complementary datasets: (1) WIKI-FADIAL, 4.2K Wikipedia-grounded dialogues for knowledge-grounded generation; (2) DAILYDIALOG-FA, 6.6K dialogues annotated for dialogue acts and emotions; and (3) PLAYDIAL-FA, 2.1K theatrical dialogues with sentiment labels. While LLMs assist data construction, every dialogue undergoes multi-stage review and revision by native Farsi speakers, and only the final human-approved dialogues are released. Experiments with six LLAMA and MISTRAL models show that LoRA substantially improves dialogue generation while requiring only 25-50% of the training data to recover over 90% of the final performance gains. Across classification tasks, FABERT achieves the best dialogue-act performance, LORA-MISTRAL-7B performs best on emotion recognition, and MISTRAL-24B achieves the highest sentiment score. Human evaluation and independent external validation demonstrate the reliability of the benchmark, while comparisons with GPT-4.1 as an LLM judge reveal that automatic metrics substantially overestimate dialogue quality. Zero-shot evaluation with frontier LLMs further shows that TalkFa remains a challenging benchmark. We will release all datasets, annotation guidelines, code, and checkpoints.

</details>


### [127] [AVERT: Audio-Verified Adjudication for Spoken Dialogue State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01828)
*Chunggi Lee,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AVERT提出了一种结合跨轮一致性投票与音频条件验证器的口语对话状态跟踪方法，通过vote、add、swap三种受限算子修复文本编辑器遗留的三种错误类型，在SpokenWOZ上将JGA从38.34提升至40.13，无需重训练基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语对话状态跟踪（DST）中ASR错误集中于实体值且跨轮持久存在，单一逐轮文本编辑器虽能修正大部分错误，但仍遗留三类可恢复错误：跨轮预测不一致的值、遗漏的槽位、以及音频不支持的值。现有方法仅依赖转录文本，未能利用音频信息和跨轮一致性来修复这些错误。

Method: AVERT框架包含两个核心组件：1）音频条件验证器，为每个候选值打分，判断其是否受音频支持；2）三种受限算子——vote（修复跨轮不一致）、add（补充遗漏槽位）、swap（替换音频不支持的槽值）。每个算子仅作用于其错误类型高发的槽位子集，通过组合跨轮一致性得分与音频验证得分来综合排序候选值。

Result: 在SpokenWOZ数据集上，基础语音-LLM达到33.04 JGA，文本编辑器提升至38.34，AVERT进一步达到40.13，超过使用完整口语历史的1B端到端系统（39.32）。音频验证器带来统计显著增益；若不加限制地让所有算子作用于全部槽位，无约束投票会覆盖正确的类别值，性能反而低于纯文本编辑器。

Conclusion: AVERT证明了将口语DST同时视为生成问题和编辑问题、并通过音频验证与槽位受限算子进行后处理修复，是一种无需重训练即可显著提升性能的有效范式。此外，结果突显了算子槽位选择的重要性——不加区分地应用投票会损害正确预测。

Abstract: Spoken dialogue state tracking recovers slot-value pairs from speech, where ASR errors concentrate in entity values and persist across turns, making it both a generation and an editing problem. A strong per-turn text editor corrects much of this but, operating on the transcript alone, leaves three recoverable errors: a value predicted inconsistently across turns, an omitted slot, and a value the audio does not support. We present AVERT, which scores each candidate value by combining cross-turn agreement with a trained audio-conditioned verifier and resolves the three error types with three operators, vote, add, and swap, each restricted to the slots where its error is common. On SpokenWOZ, a base speech-LLM reaches 33.04 JGA, a text editor 38.34, and AVERT 40.13, without retraining either. This is in the range of a 1B end-to-end system that consumes the full spoken history (39.32), though AVERT uses two 1B decoders rather than one. The audio verifier contributes a statistically significant gain, and restricting each operator to a selected slot subset matters: removing it lets unrestricted voting overwrite correct categorical values and fall below the editor.

</details>


### [128] [Interpretable Symptom Vectors for Depression in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01832)
*Fangyi Zhu,Ajay Subramanian,Allison Constant,Camille Wang,Ravish Gupta,Corey J. Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过机制可解释性技术分析Gemma-3-27B-PT模型的残差流，发现抑郁症症状在模型第21层以几何方式分离，并构建与临床医生判断一致的症状向量，可用于从自然语言中提取抑郁症状严重程度信号（AUC=0.789）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症患者临床症状表现多样，但临床实践常将其简化为单一严重度评分。大语言模型虽能捕捉患者言语中的症状及其严重程度，但其内部如何表征抑郁症状仍不清楚，限制了临床信任与应用。

Method: 使用机制可解释性技术分析Gemma-3-27B-PT的残差流；通过临床验证工具描述触发模型激活并记录各层激活；采用多种距离度量验证症状组在第21层的几何分离；利用语义投影（Semantic Projection）将天然文本投影到症状向量上获得各症状系数；构建单一抑郁向量作为情绪效价门控。

Result: 症状组在第21层几何分离最显著；逐症状系数保持临床医生标注的情绪、躯体与自杀意念轴的排序；第21层单一抑郁向量区分抑郁与非抑郁文本（AUC=0.789），可作为情绪效价门控限制症状投影。

Conclusion: 模型内部激活中存在去相关、与临床对齐的症状信号，可直接读出，为可解释的抑郁症评估工具提供了机制层面的基础。

Abstract: Patients with depression present with diverse symptom profiles, yet clinical practice routinely reduces this variation to a single severity score. Large language models (LLMs) can potentially capture various symptoms and their severity from patient speech. However, how depressive symptoms are represented inside LLMs remains poorly understood, limiting clinical trust. To examine whether internal model activations match clinician judgment, we analyzed the residual stream of Gemma-3-27B-PT using mechanistic interpretability techniques. Recording activations across symptom descriptions drawn from validated clinical instruments, we found that symptom groups geometrically separated the most at layer 21 across multiple distance metrics. Using Semantic Projection, we then projected held-out naturalistic text onto Symptom Vectors constructed from these instruments. The resulting per-symptom coefficients preserved clinician-annotated rank ordering across mood, somatic, and suicidality axes. Furthermore, a single depression vector in Layer 21 separates held-out depressive from non-depressive text (AUC = 0.789), which can be used as an emotional valence gate that restricts symptom projection to depressive speech. These results reveal a decorrelated, clinician-aligned symptom signal readable directly from internal activations, offering a mechanistic foundation for interpretable depression-assessment tools.

</details>


### [129] [Candidate Generation and Definition-Guided Verification for Sentence-Level Depression Symptom Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01833)
*Weiming Li,Catarina Barata,Miguel Constante,Joao Sanches*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，将抑郁症症状的句子级识别分解为"症状候选生成"与"基于诊断定义验证"两个环节，在多个基线上取得最优准确率与F1分数，但稀有类别性能仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句子级抑郁症症状识别具有挑战性，因为相似的表达可能对应不同的症状相关性，且语言模型的推理缺乏对诊断定义的充分锚定（grounding）。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 对比微调的句子编码器为每个句子生成症状候选；(2) 微调的语言模型结合句子、上下文及候选对应的诊断定义进行验证，在回答前先对照定义检查其判断。

Result: 在编码器、基于推理的方法、医学及通用大语言模型基线，以及匹配的单阶段监督分类器对比中，所提出的流水线在准确率和F1分数上均取得最优表现，且生成的推理依据与专家标注一致。初步临床审计表明与诊断定义中度对齐，解释质量高度依赖于预测正确性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将症状识别分解为候选生成与定义锚定验证两个阶段，但稀有症状类别的识别性能仍有待提升。

Abstract: Sentence-level recognition of depression symptoms is challenging because similar expressions can differ in symptom relevance, and language-model inference is insufficiently grounded in diagnostic definitions. This study proposes a two-stage framework separating symptom-candidate generation from definition-grounded verification. A contrastively fine-tuned sentence encoder generates a symptom candidate per sentence, and a fine-tuned language model verifies whether the candidate is present or absent using the sentence, its context, and a candidate-specific diagnostic definition, checking its judgment against that definition before answering. Evaluated against encoder, inference-based, medical, and general LLM baselines and a matched single-stage supervised classifier, the proposed pipeline attains the best accuracy and F1 scores of all methods, with rationales matching expert-authored annotations. A preliminary clinical audit indicates moderate alignment with diagnostic definitions, with explanation quality strongly dependent on prediction correctness. The results support decomposing symptom recognition into candidate generation and definition-grounded verification, though performance remains limited for rare categories.

</details>


### [130] [Cite or Decline: A Strict Course-Grounded Chatbot for STEM Lecture Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01846)
*S M Masrur Ahmed,Jaspal Subhlok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了VideoPoints平台一学期的部署，该平台为录播课视频提供基于检索增强的聊天机器人，能回答课程相关问题并返回带时间戳的引用。通过833条消息的用户研究及EduVidQA基准测试，验证了课程隔离、可追溯引用、与学习实践对齐等设计要点的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 录播课视频虽有搜索和摘要功能，但学生难以提出课程特定问题，也难以验证答案是否与讲师授课内容一致，缺乏一个能够在课程范围内提供可信回答的交互工具。

Method: 部署VideoPoints检索增强聊天机器人：(1) 仅从当前活动课程中检索；(2) 使用章节摘要指导转录排序；(3) 返回可点击的时间戳引用。通过一学期真实部署收集833条用户消息进行分析，并在EduVidQA公共基准上评估该设计，与仅使用稠密检索的基线对比。

Result: 833条消息中70.5%包含引用，无一条跨越课程边界；当无讲课证据匹配时聊天机器人通常拒绝作答而非臆测。引用是最一致有用的功能，而练习题生成是最强烈的未满足需求。在EduVidQA基准上，该方法将正确课程检索率提升6.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的部署取决于课程隔离、受支持的引用功能以及与学生学习实践的深度对齐，这为面向视频教学的检索增强问答系统提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Recorded lecture videos, often enhanced with search and summarization features, are a standard study resource. However, students cannot easily ask course specific questions or verify answers against an instructor's lecture. We report a semester-long deployment of VideoPoints platform with a retrieval-augmented chatbot that answers from course lecture materials and returns timestamped citations. The chatbot retrieves only from the active course, uses chapter summaries to guide transcript ranking, and returns clickable timestamped citations. Students used it for quick lookups and exam review. Across 833 messages, 70.5% included citations, none crossed a course boundary, and when no lecture evidence matched, the chatbot usually declined rather than answering. Among the users, citations were the most consistently useful feature, while practice-question generation was the strongest unmet request. We also evaluated the design on the real-world test split of EduVidQA, a public multimodal benchmark for lecture-video question answering. Our design improved correct-lecture retrieval by 6.3 percentage points over dense-only retrieval. Together, the results show that effective deployment depends on course isolation, supported citations, and alignment with students' study practices.

</details>


### [131] [Thinking effort aligns between humans and reasoning models in abductive reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01867)
*Henry Arthur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过溯因推理任务，考察大型推理模型（LRM）与人类在推理努力和行为上的对齐程度，发现模型与人类在推理成本及错误模式上存在相似性，且允许多路径探索的解码方法能增强这种对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知建模中的核心问题是大型语言模型与人类在语言和非语言任务上的行为对齐程度。与标准LLM不同，LRM通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习优化，鼓励正确的推理解决方案而非偏好对齐的响应。近期研究通过比较人类反应时间和模型推理轨迹来考察推理成本，但需要更严格的实验设置来验证共享努力的实证主张。

Method: 转向溯因推理任务进行对齐研究，因为与演绎任务不同，溯因推理的难度无法从形式结构中推断，且没有模型可利用来模拟努力而无需真正搜索的捷径，从而为共享努力的实证主张提供更坚实的基础。通过比较人类反应时间与模型推理轨迹，并测试允许模型探索多条推理路径的解码方法。

Result: 发现了LRM与人类推理努力对齐的进一步证据，同时发现模型和人类倾向于犯相似类型的错误。此外，允许模型探索多条推理路径的解码方法在三种被测试的模型中均增加了人类与LRM之间推理成本的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 溯因推理为考察LLM与人类推理行为对齐提供了更可靠的实验范式，研究证实LRM与人类在推理付出和错误模式上存在实质对齐，而多路径探索解码方法可进一步增强这种对齐。

Abstract: A major question in cognitive modeling concerns the behavioral alignment between large language models and humans across linguistic and non-linguistic tasks. Unlike standard LLMs, large reasoning models (LRMs) are optimized with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, encouraging correct solutions to reasoning tasks rather than preference-aligned responses. Recent work (de Varda et al., 2025) investigates the cost of thinking in humans and LRMs by comparing human reaction times with model reasoning traces across a range of reasoning tasks. We isolate this alignment by turning to abductive reasoning: unlike deductive tasks, its difficulty cannot be inferred from formal structure and offers no shortcuts a model could exploit to mimic effort without genuine search, providing firmer ground for empirical claims of shared effort. We find further evidence of alignment between LRM and human reasoning effort, as well as evidence that models and humans tend to make similar errors. Finally, we show that decoding methods that let models explore multiple reasoning paths increase alignment in reasoning cost between humans and LRMs across the three models tested.

</details>


### [132] [GAPS: Dimension-Level Gates for Conditional Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01878)
*Moghis Fereidouni,Muhammad Umair Haider,Hassan Sajjad,A. B. Siddique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GAPS方法，在激活引导中引入维度级条件化（对比现有方法仅做时间/词元级条件化），通过静态可分性门和动态后验门两个无需训练的门控机制，选择性地在哪些神经元上进行引导干预，在毒性缓解和概念移除任务上提升了帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法（如CAST、DSAS）虽然在干预时机（token级条件化）上改善了行为-能力权衡，但一旦激活，会对所有隐藏维度施加完整的稠密引导向量，无论该神经元是否携带概念信息或是否已处于期望状态。这种维度上的不加区分造成了不必要的干扰。

Method: GAPS包含两个无需训练的门控：(1) 静态可分性门——基于AUROC统计指标，仅对携带可靠概念信息的神经元进行引导；(2) 动态后验门——在高斯模型下，仅当神经元当前激活值更能被非期望概念解释时才进行引导。门控每token仅增加O(D)开销，可即插即用接入现有条件化方法（如DSAS）。

Result: 在RealToxicityPrompts（毒性缓解）和OneSeC（概念移除）任务上，使用Gemma-3(4B)和Qwen-3(1.7B)评估，GAPS一致匹配或改善了其token级对应方法的帕累托前沿。在固定能力预算下，DSAS+GAPS将Gemma-3的毒性率从6.52%降至0.48%，而单独的DSAS仅降至3.52%。消融实验表明大部分增益来自后验门。

Conclusion: 维度级条件化是与时间级条件化正交且互补的选择性轴，GAPS证明了在激活引导中加入"选择干预哪些神经元"的维度选择性可以显著提升行为-能力权衡，且方法轻量、训练免费、可插拔，具有良好实用性。

Abstract: Activation steering suppresses undesired behaviors in language models by adding a steering vector to the hidden state during generation. Recent conditional methods such as CAST and DSAS improve the behavior-capability trade-off by deciding when to intervene, but once active, they apply the full dense vector to all hidden dimensions, regardless of whether a neuron carries concept information or already lies in the desired regime. We introduce dimension-level conditioning as a complementary axis of selectivity that also decides which neurons to intervene on. Our method, GAPS (Gated Activation steering via Posterior and Separability), combines two training-free gates: a static separability gate that restricts steering to neurons with statistically reliable concept information (via AUROC), and a dynamic posterior gate that steers a neuron only when its current activation is better explained by the undesired concept under a Gaussian model. The gates add O(D) overhead per token, and they plug into existing conditional methods. On toxicity mitigation (RealToxicityPrompts) and concept removal (OneSeC) with Gemma-3 (4B) and Qwen-3 (1.7B), GAPS consistently matches or improves the Pareto front of its token-level counterparts; under a fixed capability budget, DSAS+GAPS reduces Gemma-3's toxicity rate from 6.52% to 0.48%, versus 3.52% for DSAS alone. Ablations attribute most of the gain to the posterior gate.

</details>


### [133] [Grounded, Compute-Efficient LLM Policy Agents for Energy-Poverty Equity in Physically-Constrained Peer-to-Peer Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01918)
*Kunal Jadhav,Siddhesh More*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EqGrid，一个闭环仿真系统，结合低频率开源权重LLM策略智能体（设定电价、碳上限和补贴）与高频多智能体RL交易者（在IEEE-33节点电网物理约束下清算连续双向拍卖），用于解决能源贫困问题。系统通过人本模拟评估社会影响，并证明压缩LLM模型可在保持大部分公平性收益的同时大幅降低推理能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源贫困在NLP-for-social-good领域几乎未被涉及，现有工作要么是静态检索/问答，要么依赖高碳排放的云端LLM——这对人道主义场景而言是自相矛盾的"计算反讽"。作者希望填补这一空白，并以低计算成本方式衡量AI的社会影响。

Method: EqGrid包含：1) 区域匹配的经验性家庭人设（负载曲线用真实智能电表数据校验）；2) 形式化能源贫困公平性指标（Energy Burden、EB的Gini系数、LIHC）；3) 计算效率前沿，衡量从235B教师模型压缩到子1B设备端模型的公平性性能保留程度。采用解耦安全设计：LLM设定边界，验证-投影电网门控执行，物理电网为IEEE-33节点含动态运行包络。

Result: 解耦安全设计实现零电网约束违规（直接LLM控制下为55次违规）。LLM策略将能源负担Gini从0.351降至0.305，平均负担降低28%，同时削减成本（优于规则基线）。3B激活模型保留95%收益，推理能耗约为教师模型的1/9；0.8B设备端模型保留92%收益，能耗约为1/24。

Conclusion: EqGrid展示了开源、轻量级LLM策略agent可以有效用于能源贫困缓解，同时保持物理安全和极高的计算效率，为衡量AI社会影响提供了新的范式和开源工具。

Abstract: Energy poverty is nearly absent from NLP-for-social-good, and the little existing work is either static retrieval/QA or relies on carbon-intensive cloud LLMs, a self-defeating "computational irony" for a humanitarian setting. We present EqGrid, a closed-loop simulation in which a low-frequency, open-weight LLM policy agent sets price and carbon bounds and targeted subsidies over a community of empirically-grounded household personas, while high-frequency multi-agent RL traders clear a continuous double auction constrained by a physical distribution grid (IEEE-33-bus with Dynamic Operating Envelopes). Our contribution is threefold and directly addresses how to measure the social impact of AI: (i) grounded personas (region-matched socio-demographics) whose load curves are checked for shape and level realism against real smart-meter data; (ii) formal energy-poverty equity metrics (Energy Burden, Gini of EB, LIHC) showing the intervention reduces burden inequality without raising net grid cost; and (iii) a compute-efficiency frontier that measures how much equity performance survives compressing the policy agent from a 235B teacher down to a sub-1B model deployable on a laptop, in estimated energy/carbon per decision. A decoupled-safety design (the LLM sets bounds; a validate-and-project grid gate executes) yields zero grid-constraint violations versus 55 under direct LLM control. On energy-poverty equity, the LLM policy lowers the Gini of energy burden to 0.305 (from 0.351) and mean burden by 28% while cutting cost (outperforming a tuned rule baseline), and a 3B-active model retains 95% of the benefit at roughly 9x lower inference energy than the teacher, with even a 0.8B on-device model retaining 92% at roughly 24x lower energy. We will release code and configs.

</details>


### [134] [Sparse Readout Prism: Explaining Logit-Lens Scores in Features Instead of Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01936)
*Matteo He,William F. Shen,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Sparse Readout Prism (SRP)的方法，用于分解语言模型的readout（unembedding矩阵），以独立于拟合语料库的方式分析隐藏状态到token的映射过程。SRP仅使用readout权重进行稀疏分解，将token logit表示为稀疏readout特征的贡献之和，克服了lens方法中"corpus conditionality"（语料库条件性）问题，提供了一种控制拟合语料库偏差的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lens方法通过解码中间隐藏状态来追踪语言模型在各层对next token的预测，但lens读数同时反映隐藏状态和用于解码的readout。许多lens在语料库上拟合，研究发现仅拟合语料不同的两个lens会对相同隐藏状态报告不同token，这种依赖性被称为corpus conditionality。因此需要一种不受拟合语料库影响的方式来分析readout结构。

Method: 提出Sparse Readout Prism (SRP)，仅使用readout的权重对其进行稀疏分解，将任意token logit或logit差表示为稀疏readout特征的贡献之和。该方法不依赖任何语料库，利用readout行之间的几何/结构关系来发现可解释的readout特征，实现跨token、上下文、层和lens的比较。

Result: SRP的稀疏近似在重建测试logit差异方面比六个基于readout行几何关系的基线方法高出8.9-17.3个百分点。消融特征会使logit差异按SRP贡献比例偏移。虽然token读数随拟合语料库变化，但主导的readout特征保持稳定。

Conclusion: readout特征可作为lens读数分析的新分析单元，揭示token身份难以展现的结构。由于SRP构造过程不使用语料库，它提供了一种独立于拟合语料库的控制方法，适用于lens分析，解决了corpus conditionality问题。

Abstract: A language model's prediction of its next token develops across layers, and lens methods track this process by decoding intermediate hidden states into tokens. But a lens reading reflects both the hidden state and the readout (the unembedding matrix) used to decode it. Many lenses are fit on a corpus, and we show that two lenses differing only in their fitting corpus can report different tokens for the same hidden states. We call this dependence corpus conditionality. To examine readout structure independently of the fitting corpus, we introduce Sparse Readout Prism (SRP), which decomposes the readout using only its weights and expresses any token logit or logit difference as a sum of contributions from sparse readout features. This reveals readout features as a new unit of analysis for lens readings, exposing structure that token identities can obscure and enabling comparisons across tokens, contexts, layers, and lenses. Replacing the original readout with SRP's sparse approximation reconstructs 8.9-17.3 percentage points more of the tested logit differences than the strongest of six baselines built on geometric relations among readout rows. Ablating features shifts logit differences in proportion to their SRP contributions. Although token readings vary with the fitting corpus, the dominant readout feature remains stable. Because SRP uses no corpus in its construction, it provides a control independent of the fitting corpus for lens analyses.

</details>


### [135] [NS-Copilot: An LLM-Driven Agent System for Autonomous Neuroscience Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01971)
*Wuche Liu,Yiran Qiao,Linlin Hou,Rui Yang,Shusen Pu,Song Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NS-Copilot是一个由大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统，用于神经科学数据分析。它通过自然语言接口整合了特定领域的预训练模型，支持EEG和细胞外尖峰数据等关键神经科学模态，能够自主完成端到端的专业分析流程。系统在阿尔茨海默病、帕金森病和工作记忆尖峰解码等神经科学基准测试中持续优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能虽在快速推动神经科学进步，但许多实验室因显著的跨学科壁垒未能充分发挥其潜力。尽管神经科学领域的预训练模型发展迅速，但其异构架构和模态特定限制阻碍了系统性集成、选择和评估。现有的基于LLM的智能体系统缺乏有效选择与协调多种神经科学预训练模型及处理该领域独特数据类型所需的领域专业知识。

Method: NS-Copilot采用LLM驱动的多智能体架构，统一了领域特定的预训练模型，支持EEG和细胞外尖峰数据等关键神经科学模态，通过自然语言界面实现交互。系统为规划、自适应控制、代码生成和结果综合等环节编排具有专业角色的智能体，使分析无需依赖数据集特定的启发式方法即可完成。

Result: 在跨越阿尔茨海默病、帕金森病和工作记忆尖峰解码的神经科学基准测试中，每个任务进行8次试验，NS-Copilot在主要评估指标上持续优于强基线方法，展现了其在神经科学分析中的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: NS-Copilot证明了LLM驱动的多智能体系统能够有效克服神经科学数据分析中的跨学科壁垒，实现专业预训练模型的系统集成与协调，为神经科学实验室提供可扩展、无需数据集特定启发式的端到端分析解决方案。

Abstract: AI is rapidly advancing neuroscience, yet many laboratories fail to fully unleash its potential due to significant interdisciplinary barriers. While pre-trained neural models for physiological data are progressing quickly, their heterogeneous architectures and modality-specific constraints hinder systematic integration, selection, and evaluation. Despite recent advances in large language model (LLM)-based agent systems for intelligent scientific applications, existing approaches often still lack the domain expertise required to effectively select and coordinate diverse neuroscience pre-trained models and handle unique data types in this domain. We present NS-Copilot, an LLM-driven multi-agent system for neuroscience analysis that autonomously supports end-to-end workflows for diverse professional tasks. It unifies domain-specific pre-trained models and supports key neuroscience modalities, including EEG and extracellular spike data, through a natural-language interface. Given raw data and a task description, NS-Copilot orchestrates agents with specialized roles for planning, adaptive control, code generation, and result synthesis, enabling analysis without dataset-specific heuristics. We evaluate NS-Copilot on neuroscience benchmarks spanning Alzheimer's disease, Parkinson's disease, and working memory spike decoding. Across 8 trials per task, the system consistently outperforms strong baselines on the primary metric, demonstrating the ability of NS-Copilot for effective and scalable neuroscience analysis.

</details>


### [136] [How Output Format Confounds Data Quality and Capability in Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02015)
*Chengguang Gan,Hanjun Wei,Yunhao Liang,Qinghao Zhang,Shiwen Ni,Zhixi Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过12个任务、四种语义等价的输出界面、三个模型家族及受控破坏实验，利用梯度签名证明输出格式（界面）会混淆数据质量与模型能力的测量。谱统计（如有效秩）对界面旋转不变但对语义破坏不敏感，而更新方向携带质量信号。能力本身是相对于训练界面存储的：同一技能在训练格式下提升40分以上，在其他格式下几乎不可见；仅修正生成预算即可使GSM8K上微调的效果从增益翻转为大幅损失。数据质量与模型能力是界面条件化的量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践分别用质量指标评判指令微调数据、用基准评判微调模型，但这两类判断都经由同一输出界面（答案的书面格式）传递。作者怀疑这一中间环节被忽视，从而系统性地混淆了数据质量与模型能力的测量。

Method: 在12个任务上，构造四种语义等价的输出格式（界面），跨越三个模型家族进行梯度签名分析；引入受控语义破坏；使用有效秩等谱统计及更新方向分析；通过预设（pre-registered）干预实验界定该几何结构的控制边界。

Result: ①谱统计对界面旋转不变、对语义破坏却失明，而更新方向携带质量信号；②界面引起的残差并非噪声——它能跨三个模型家族完美识别该单元自身的任务；③能力相对训练界面存储：训练格式下提升>40分的能力在其他格式下几乎不可见；④修正单个生成预算就使GSM8K微调效果从收益翻转为大额损失。

Conclusion: 数据质量与模型能力本质上是界面条件化的量，当前的评测实践往往报告的是界面而非内容本身。预设干预实验表明该几何结构在实际控制层面存在边界。研究呼吁评测需将输出界面作为条件变量加以考虑。

Abstract: Instruction-tuning data are judged by quality metrics, and tuned models are judged by benchmarks, but both judgments pass through an output interface: the surface format in which an answer is written. Using gradient signatures across 12 tasks, four semantically equivalent interfaces, three model families, and controlled corruptions, we show that this interface confounds both measurements. Spectral statistics such as effective rank are provably invariant to interface rotation and empirically blind to semantic corruption, while the direction of the update carries the quality signal. The interface-varying residual is not noise: it identifies each unit's own target task perfectly across all three families. Capability itself is stored relative to the training interface: a skill that raises accuracy by more than 40 points under the training format can be nearly invisible under every other, and correcting a single generation budget flips the measured effect of fine-tuning on GSM8K from a gain into a large loss. Pre-registered interventions delimit where this geometry stops short of control. Data quality and model capability are interface-conditioned quantities, and current practice often reports the interface instead of the content.

</details>


### [137] [A Tri-Agent Framework for Evaluating and Aligning Question Clarification Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02054)
*Yikai Zhao,Saurabh Pandey,Pradeep Kumar Misra*

Main category: cs.CL
