Method: 以一位患有严重构音障碍的中风后匈牙利语男性说话人为对象，在两个生产级基座（Whisper-large-v3和Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）上比较七种LoRA家族方法（LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA），采用配对bootstrap显著性检验及6点注册数据量扫描评估性能-数据量权衡。

Result: LoRA与DoRA无显著差异（p>0.5）；真实4-bit QLoRA在所有种子和基座上均更差且无内存节省；LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA和AdaLoRA不及LoRA家族。全量微调更准确（11.43% CER），但115MB的LoRA在适配前馈模块后仅差0.66个百分点，存储仅为全量的3.7%；5分钟音频可捕获零样本到30分钟CER改善的45.6%。

Conclusion: 在逐病人构音障碍ASR场景下，简单且存储高效的LoRA是性价比最优的选择；少量患者音频即可获得大部分性能增益，研究为临床部署提供了实用指导，但结论需注意其局限于单说话人、单语言、严重中风后构音障碍的适用范围。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出声明式注意力（Declarative Attention, DA），一种让模型在思维链中主动声明需要关注哪些上下文的协议，将生成分为全局、聚焦、局部三种模式，从而在推理时跳过大部分KV缓存读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型将大部分注意力集中在少数上下文token上，但现有方法仍需扫描完整KV缓存。虽然已有通过轻量级代理分数预选相关token的方法，但其外在评分仍需要O(N)的每步开销。作者提出核心问题：模型自身是否已经知道需要关注哪些上下文？

Method: DA协议让模型在思维链中主动声明需要关注的区域，生成被划分为三种模式：<global>（全上下文）、<focus>（特定区域）、<local>（仅最近输出）。推理引擎像解析工具调用一样解析这些声明，从而跳过大部分KV缓存读取。

Result: 在15个长上下文任务的零样本评估中，DA应用于现成模型（Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B）显著减少了解码期间的注意力token总数（分别减少52.0%和31.1%），精度仅有小幅下降（1.27pp和2.75pp），且精度损失随模型规模增大而缩小。

Conclusion: DA开创了稀疏注意力的新维度，无需训练即可在现成模型上实现显著的计算节省，未来结合基于训练的方法有望进一步释放潜力。

Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨误导性上下文如何影响大语言模型的医疗判断。在MedMisBench基准（8,627道题）上注入两类误导线索（伪造证据和赤裸断言），发现三个推理模型对断言比伪造证据更易受影响（高10-27个百分点），且断言更少被披露。两种误导线索的机制不同：证据早期累积进入，断言在结论前重定向推理。开放推理轨迹配合LLM监控器可捕获78%的腐败决策（5%误报率），远超任何响应的32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在医疗问答上已达到专家级表现，但它们所处的上下文可能具有误导性，而这种误导性上下文会损害模型的医疗判断。理解这种腐败机制对评估模型在真实医疗场景中的可靠性至关重要，因为提供OOD（超出分布）的误导信息在临床环境中十分常见。

Method: 在MedMisBench基准的医疗推理子集上，注入两类误导上下文线索：伪造证据（fabricated evidence）和赤裸断言（bare assertion）。测试三个推理模型：两个暴露完整推理轨迹，一个前沿模型仅暴露响应。通过重采样分析推理轨迹，研究误导的进入时机与累积机制，并使用LLM监控器评估腐败决策的可检测性。

Result: 所有三个模型对断言比伪造证据更易受影响（高出10-27个百分点）。误导线索在81-98%的轨迹中被披露，但仅7-90%的响应中披露，断言比证据类线索披露更少。两种线索腐败推理机制不同：证据早期进入并逐渐累积，断言在结论阶段重定向推理。LLM监控器在读取开放模型的推理轨迹时，以5%误报率捕获78%的腐败决策，而任何响应方式最多仅捕获32%。

Conclusion: 模型最易受影响的误导上下文类型（赤裸断言）反而披露最少，且只有在开放的推理轨迹中才能被可靠监控捕获，而前沿模型提供商往往不提供这种开放轨迹。这表明模型鲁棒性、透明性和可监督性之间存在张力，对医疗等高风险领域的模型部署具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出受影响函数引导的响应重写（influence-guided response rewriting），用于检验训练数据归因（TDA）的干预价值。研究发现，与传统的权重重加权干预相比，对同一批影响函数选中的样本进行响应重写（而非修改其权重）能产生更强、更持久且双向的行为改变，从而区分了影响函数捕捉的局部重加权效应与这些样本本身具备的更广泛干预杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影响函数（IF）往往能识别有影响力的训练样本，但在传统的基于权重重加权（reweighting）干预下，IF选中的样本相比随机选择并无明显优势。这引发疑问：到底是这些有影响力样本本身缺乏干预价值，还是重加权干预方式未能发挥其行为杠杆作用？作者希望厘清这一矛盾。

Method: 作者引入影响引导的响应重写方法：使用影响函数识别干预目标样本，然后固定指令（instruction）不变，将选定样本的响应替换为与目标行为一致或相反的监督信号。在四个开源大语言模型上，以认知性弃权（epistemic abstention）为主要测试场景，比较了响应重写与权重重加权在同一批影响函数选定样本上的效果。

Result: 1) 响应重写比权重重加权产生更强、更持久且双向的行为变化，而重加权效果弱且不一致；2) 影响函数选中的样本比其它选择器提供更大的重写杠杆；3) 行为变化集中在目标相关行为上；4) 相同的定性对比可推广到安全拒绝（safety refusal）任务。

Conclusion: 影响函数估计所捕捉的局部重加权效应，与这些样本本身具有的更广泛干预杠杆是两回事。该方法论的区分凸显了训练数据归因评估需要考虑干预方式（intervention-aware evaluation），而非仅考察选择器本身。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperStyler，一种新型的低资源作者风格迁移（LAST）架构，将风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现两个阶段。Stylo-navigator通过联合建模源上下文与目标作者参考来预测风格坐标，Stylo-hypernet通过动态参数调制而非隐藏状态注入来实现风格。在Reddit、Blog和News数据集上，HyperStyler持续优于包括LLM在内的现有方法，仅需T5-large 2.4%的额外参数，推理速度比LLM快1.8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAST方法存在两大缺陷：一是将多样化的参考样本压缩为单一静态作者嵌入，平均化了上下文相关的风格变化；二是依赖隐藏表示进行风格控制，导致风格与内容纠缠不清，难以同时实现高风格保真度和语义保留。

Method: 提出HyperStyler架构，将LAST解耦为两个模块：1) Stylo-navigator：联合建模源文本上下文和目标作者参考样例，预测风格坐标；2) Stylo-hypernet：通过动态参数调制（而非隐藏状态注入）实现风格，实现风格与内容的彻底解耦。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News三个数据集上，HyperStyler一致优于包括基于LLM的方法在内的先前方法，并展现出跨领域的稳健泛化能力。仅需T5-large 2.4%的额外参数即可取得优异性能，推理速度比LLM快1.8倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过将风格选择与风格实现解耦，并以参数调制替代隐藏状态注入，HyperStyler有效解决了低资源作者风格迁移中风格保真度与语义保持难以兼顾的挑战，同时保持了参数和计算效率。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EarlyEval，一个轻量级框架，通过在任务执行过程中利用 LightGBM 分类器对代理的中间行为进行早期结果预测，一旦达到置信度阈值即提前终止运行，从而在保留评估保真度的同时显著降低 LLM 代理评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理的评估成本极其高昂，前沿模型在代理基准上单次评估可能花费数百至数千美元，且在迭代开发周期中需反复支付。现有工作集中于基准蒸馏以减少评估任务数量，但未触及每个任务内部的执行成本。

Method: 提出早期结果预测（early outcome prediction）这一与基准蒸馏互补的效率维度。核心洞察是代理的最终结果往往在其执行完成前就能从中间行为中显现。据此实现 EarlyEval 框架：训练一对 LightGBM 成功与失败分类器，使用行为、文本和参考解特征，当任一分类器越过校准置信度阈值时立即终止代理运行，每个步骤仅增加可忽略的开销。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench 和 Toolathlon 三个基准上，EarlyEval 可消除 13%-26% 的代理步骤，最多节省 44.1% 的输入 token 和 29.4% 的输出 token，预测准确率达 89%-97%，同时仅对每个代理的 resolve rate 产生平均 1-2 个百分点的扰动。

Conclusion: 早期结果预测提供了一条在任务内部削减评估成本的新路径，与基准蒸馏互补。EarlyEval 在大量降低计算成本的同时保持了评估结果的可靠性，为经济高效的 LLM 代理评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [Two Centuries of Sexism in British Parliament: A Computational Analysis of Women's Representation in the Hansard Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30485)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Mandira Sawkar,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析英国议会200年间（1803-2005）关于妇女权利的辩论演讲，使用大语言模型分类演讲者对妇女选举权的立场并分析性别歧视言论，发现反对妇女代表权的演讲中有54%含性别歧视内容（相比支持方21%），且两方使用不同类型的性别歧视（敌对性vs.善意性），并发布了6.7百万篇演讲的议会数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究立法机构中辩论妇女权利的性别歧视模式如何跨两个世纪持续存在，检验Ambivalent Sexism Theory在真实政治话语中的适用性，并提供一个面向计算社会科学研究的、经过组织和元数据增强的议会辩论数据集。

Method: 使用大语言模型对6,531篇跨越200年英国议会辩论演讲进行分类，包括对妇女选举权和政治代表权的立场分类，以及基于Ambivalent Sexism Inventory对性别歧视言论进行分析；同时发布了包含6.7百万篇演讲、1.2百万场辩论的Hansard数据集（89%性别匹配率）。

Result: 发现54%反对妇女代表权的演讲含性别歧视内容，而支持方仅为21%；反选举权言论同时使用敌对性和善意性框架，而支持选举权的性别歧视几乎完全是善意性的；女议员支持妇女政治权利的比例为93%，高于男议员的70%。

Conclusion: 研究结果为Ambivalent Sexism Theory提供了实证支持，证明敌对性和善意性性别歧视在不同修辞语境中被以与该理论一致的方式使用，并揭示了即使在支持妇女权利的话语中仍存在系统性性别歧视的深层社会现象。

Abstract: The language a legislature uses to debate women's rights, even in favour of them, encodes systematic patterns of sexism that persist across two centuries. In this work, we analyse 6,531 speeches over 200 years of UK parliamentary debate (Hansard, 1803-2005) by using large language models to classify a speaker's perspective towards women's suffrage and political representation, as well as analyse sexist speech in parliament from the lens of the Ambivalent Sexism Inventory. We also release this parliamentary dataset, an organized and metadata-enriched version of the publicly available Hansard Corpus optimized for computational social science research, with 6.7 million speeches across 1.2 million debates, with 89% gender-matching for speeches by MPs from the House of Commons. We find that 54% of speeches opposing women's representation contain sexist content, compared to 21% of speeches that are for the cause, and that the two sides use fundamentally different types of sexism: anti-suffrage rhetoric combines hostile and benevolent framing, while pro-suffrage sexism is overwhelmingly benevolent. Female MPs support women's political rights at 93% compared to 70% for male MPs, a gap that closes only after enfranchisement. Our findings are evidence that benevolent and hostile sexism are used in different rhetorical contexts in a manner consistent with the theory of Ambivalent Sexism.

</details>


### [179] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiscoSign，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化框架，用于话语感知的文本到手语注释翻译。它解决了三个关键的话语级现象：空间共指消解、问答从句（QACs）和概念-注释一致性，并引入了一套新的评估指标来度量话语级翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅在句子层面运行，忽视了手语理解中至关重要的语篇现象。这些系统无法处理跨句的空间一致性、特殊的句式结构等功能，导致翻译缺乏话语层面的连贯性。

Method: 提出一个基于LLM的模块化翻译框架DiscoSign，针对三个话语级现象分别设计处理模块：(i)空间共指消解，确保实体在整个话语中保持一致的空间位置；(ii)问答从句（QACs）这类伪分裂结构的处理；(iii)概念-注释一致性映射。同时引入一套新型评估指标来分别衡量每个话语连贯性维度。

Result: 在句子级和话语级数据集上的实验表明，话语感知处理方法在空间一致性和实体追踪方面相比仅句子翻译有显著提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的单句注释翻译质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个系统性的、带配套评估方法的话语级文本到手语注释翻译框架，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [180] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"用户反馈噪声大、难以有效利用"的既有观点，通过合成数据（有确定真相）和自然数据两种设置，对比有无反馈的模型修订效果，证明反馈驱动的修订能显著更有效地解决目标问题。同时揭示当前评估范式存在系统性偏差：当模型仅凭反馈成功修正问题时，LLM评判者往往无法识别真正的改进，反而偏好较差的基线输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用用户交互中的自然反馈作为LLM的学习信号具有很大潜力，但近期研究表明这种反馈本质上充满噪声且难以有效利用。作者希望推翻这一悲观论断，并找出"反馈似乎无效"的真正原因——究竟是反馈本身无用，还是评估范式存在偏差。

Method: 1) 构建具有确定性真实标签的合成数据来隔离反馈的效用；2) 同时构建自然主义数据以验证结论在真实场景中成立；3) 在两种设置下，对比"有反馈"与"无反馈"的模型修订表现。

Result: 反馈驱动的修订在解决目标问题上的成功率显著高于无反馈的基线修订。此外，作者发现评估偏差的根源在于：当模型仅因反馈而成功修复问题时，LLM评判者频繁无法识别真正被纠正的答案，反而系统性地偏好较差的基线输出。

Conclusion: 用户反馈实际上是高度可操作的有效改进信号，其此前被认为无效的原因在于评估范式的系统性偏差，而非反馈本身的价值有限。这提示研究界需要重新审视现有的LLM评估方法。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [PRISM: An Agentic Multi-Model Architecture for Proactive Safety in Autonomous Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01623)
*Joyjit Roy,Samaresh Kumar Singh,Sushanta Das*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PRISM是一种智能体多模型安全架构，通过逆向碰撞概率建模将二元碰撞分类器转化为动态可解释的安全评分，结合强化学习、上下文记忆和特征级归因，在运行时提供从静默监测到紧急警报的四级渐进式安全干预。在1,296个真实驾驶场景中无需数据集特定重训练即可实现平均68/100的安全评分和3.8%的近碰撞率识别，实现了从反应式碰撞规避到主动式持续风险管理的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ADAS系统采用反应式机制，仅在危险出现后才激活，这在美国弱势道路使用者（VRU）伤亡率持续上升的背景下构成严重局限。现有基于规则的安全系统使用静态阈值，无法适应动态复杂的城市交通环境，需要一种从被动反应转向主动持续风险管理的架构。

Method: PRISM采用逆向碰撞概率建模将二元碰撞分类器转换为动态可解释安全分数；三个专用模型分别处理轨迹运动学、环境风险和VRU交互，并并行运行；一个包含强化学习、上下文记忆和特征级归因的推理层用于协调这些模型；系统通过四个层级（静默监测到紧急警报）提供渐进式安全干预，并在运行时动态调整安全参数而非使用静态阈值。

Result: 在三个自然驾驶数据集共1,296个场景中验证且无需数据集特定重训练，平均安全评分为68/100，77.6%场景被分类为建议级，近碰撞率为3.8%，11%场景升级为干预或紧急响应。特征归因一致识别出轨迹风险和VRU接近度为主要安全因素。

Conclusion: PRISM提供了一个统一、可解释的主动式交通安全框架，特别关注密集城市环境中的VRU风险降低，证明了无需数据集特定重训练即可实现跨数据集的泛化能力，并实现了从反应式到主动式安全管理的范式转变。

Abstract: Autonomous and intelligent transportation systems operate in complex urban environments where safety depends on interactions among vehicle behavior, environmental conditions, and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. Most advanced driver assistance systems (ADAS) employ reactive mechanisms that activate only after hazards have emerged, a critical limitation underscored by rising VRU fatalities in the United States.
  This study introduces PRISM (Proactive Risk Intelligence and Safety Management), an agentic multi-model safety architecture that transitions from reactive crash avoidance to proactive, continuous risk management. PRISM employs inverse crash-probability modeling to convert binary crash classifiers into dynamic, interpretable safety scores. Three specialized models addressing trajectory kinematics, environmental risk, and VRU interaction operate concurrently, coordinated by a reasoning layer incorporating reinforcement learning, contextual memory, and feature-level attribution. The system provides graduated safety interventions across four tiers, from silent monitoring to emergency alerts.
  Unlike rule-based systems with static thresholds, PRISM dynamically adjusts safety parameters in real time. Validated across 1,296 scenarios from three naturalistic driving datasets without dataset-specific retraining, the system yielded a mean safety score of 68 out of 100, classified 77.6% of scenarios as advisory, and flagged a near-miss rate of 3.8%, with 11% of scenarios escalating to intervention or emergency response. Feature attribution consistently identified trajectory risk and VRU proximity as primary safety factors. PRISM provides a unified, interpretable framework for proactive transportation safety with emphasis on VRU risk reduction in dense urban environments.

</details>


### [182] [Differential Games for Compositional Handling of Competing Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01838)
*Joshua Shay Kricheli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微分博弈的分治(Divide and Conquer)控制设计方法，用于单智能体多目标动态系统。每个控制目标关联一个虚拟输入并建立非合作微分博弈，通过求解纳什均衡合成复合控制器，在冲突目标间实现稳定平衡。作者提供了连续时间和离散时间系统的形式化推导，开发了开源Python包求解无穷时域微分博弈中的耦合代数Riccati方程，并在倒立摆和四旋翼两个基准案例上与经典LQR对比，展示了更优的轨迹跟踪和鲁棒多目标调节性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模单智能体系统中，动态冲突的控制目标使得全局权重调节(global weighting)难以处理(intractable)，传统方法缺乏直观且模块化的框架来平衡多个相互竞争的控制目标。

Method: 将每个控制目标与一个虚拟输入关联，建立非合作有限/无限时域微分博弈；每位玩家(代表一个目标)基于自身虚拟代价函数优化，同时考虑其他玩家的最优策略；通过建立纳什均衡合成复合控制器；提供连续与离散时间系统形式化推导，并开发求解耦合代数Riccati方程的数值算法。

Result: 在倒立摆(cart-pole)和非线性分层控制四旋翼两个基准案例上，与经典LQR相比，该方法在多种瞬态和稳态控制指标上展现出更优的轨迹跟踪和鲁棒多目标调节性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为控制工程师提供了一种直观、模块化的框架，便于在整个设计周期中进行参数重新调优，有效解决多目标动态冲突问题，且已通过开源Python包进行验证。

Abstract: We introduce a novel Divide and Conquer control design methodology leveraging differential games in single-agent, multi-objective dynamical systems. The proposed framework associates each control objective with a virtual input and establishes a non-cooperative, finite or infinite horizon differential game among representative players. Each player optimizes a distinct virtual cost function tailored to its specific goal, the full system state, and the other virtual inputs, while accounting for the remaining players' optimal policies. By establishing a Nash Equilibrium for this game, we synthesize a composite controller that achieves a stable balance across competing objectives, providing control engineers with an intuitive and modular framework for parameter re-tuning throughout the design cycle. We provide formal mathematical derivations for both continuous-time and discrete-time dynamical systems, targeting large-scale single-agent applications where complex, dynamically conflicting control objectives make global weighting intractable. To demonstrate the methodology, we developed an open-source Python package implementing a novel numerical algorithm for solving Coupled Algebraic Riccati Equations arising in infinite-horizon differential games. We evaluate the approach on two benchmark case studies: an inverted pendulum on a cart and a non-linear hierarchically controlled quadrotor. The resulting closed-loop performance is compared against the classical Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) across various transient and steady-state control metrics, demonstrating superior trajectory tracking and robust multi-objective regulation.

</details>


### [183] [ArcticSwarm: Deferring Early Consensus in Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01870)
*Soyoung Yoon,Boyi Liu,Yite Wang,Ruofan Wu,Canwen Xu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Seung-won Hwang,Yuxiong He,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ArcticSwarm 是一种多智能体深度研究架构，通过分离证据收集与证据整合、利用门控隔离防止早期共识、并在三个承诺边界实施结构化审查，显著提升了开放式长时程研究任务的性能（BrowseComp-Plus 达 82.6%，超越基线方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在具备可靠验证器的领域（如代码生成）表现优异，但开放式、长时程的研究任务缺乏验证器。多数投票或自一致性作为替代验证器时，并行智能体反复探索相同证据，而同伴的部分发现会导致搜索过早收敛于早期候选方案，尚未充分测试其他替代方案。

Method: ArcticSwarm 架构将证据收集与证据整合分离：子智能体将发现发布到共享公告板，同时采用门控隔离机制让选定的搜索任务保持自身先验，防止早期共识；并在三个承诺边界实施结构化审查，仅将高置信度候选方案向前传播。

Result: 在完整 BrowseComp-Plus 数据集上，使用开源 Qwen 3.5-27B 模型达到 82.6% 准确率（无门控隔离为 78.8%，额外禁用结构化审查为 74.5%），优于 MiroFlow 基线（70.6%）。在实时网页 BrowseComp 上以 GPT-5 达到 73.6%，远高于提供方系统（54.9%）和 MiroFlow（63.4%）。

Conclusion: 在证据收集阶段限制同伴读取，并在假设共享前强化承诺边界，可以拓宽搜索空间并提升长时程多智能体深度研究的性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems have shown strong performance in domains with reliable verifiers such as coding, where multi-parallel candidate generation selected by a verifier is effective. However, such pipelines would not generalize to open-ended, long-horizon research tasks without a verifier. While majority voting or self-consistency is often used to reach consensus as a proxy verifier, parallel agents repeatedly explore the same evidence, while access to peers' partial findings cause search to converge on an early candidate before alternatives are tested. We present ArcticSwarm, a multi-agent research architecture that separates evidence gathering from evidence integration. Subagents publish findings to a shared bulletin board, while gated isolation lets selected search tasks maintain their own prior, preventing early consensus. Structured review at three commitment boundaries enforce only confident candidates to be propagated. As a result, ArcticSwarm reaches 82.6% on the full BrowseComp-Plus set with the open-weight Qwen 3.5-27B model, compared with 78.8% without gated isolation and 74.5% additionally with structured review disabled, outperforming aligned baseline MiroFlow runs (70.6%). Extending to live-web BrowseComp, ArcticSwarm reaches 73.6% with GPT-5, which is well above the reported provider system (54.9%) and MiroFlow (63.4%). Overall, the results show that restricting peer reads during evidence gathering and strengthening commitment boundaries before a hypothesis is shared can broaden search and improve long-horizon multi-agent deep research.

</details>


### [184] [RideSkill: A Hierarchical Algorithm for Generalized Ride Sharing with LLM-Driven Automatic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02250)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: RideSkill是一个用于拼车（ride-sharing）问题的层次化方法，利用LLM辅助的自动算法设计。它包含一个combiner（为每辆车从技能库中分配合适技能）和一个repositioner（将空闲车辆重新定位到新兴需求区域），三者均由基于LLM的自动进化方法训练，部署时无需LLM调用，保证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MARL拼车方案存在泛化能力有限、难以适应不同平台目标以及大规模系统中的训练困难等问题。虽然已有工作将LLM引入打车系统，但它们都不支持车辆共享（拼车），且大多需要频繁的LLM推理调用，无法满足实时部署需求。

Method: 提出RideSkill层次化架构：(1) combiner从学习到的技能库中为每辆车分配合适的技能，实现在不同场景和目标下的自适应调度；(2) repositioner顺序地将空闲车辆重新定位到新兴需求区域，避免车辆间冲突；(3) 技能库、combiner和repositioner均由LLM驱动的自动进化方法训练，部署时无需LLM调用。

Result: RideSkill通过LLM辅助的自动算法设计克服了状态和动作空间指数级扩大的问题，训练完成后部署阶段不需要LLM调用，确保了高实时性能。据摘要所述该方法能够适应不同场景和目标，避免车辆冲突。具体实验结果未在摘要中呈现。

Conclusion: RideSkill提供了一种将LLM能力与自动算法设计相结合的拼车调度方案，在训练阶段利用LLM的进化搜索能力，同时在部署阶段完全脱离LLM依赖，兼顾了算法的灵活性与实时性。

Abstract: Ride-sharing, which allows multiple passengers with different origin-destination (OD) pairs to share a single vehicle, is a challenging operational problem, as it requires orders with different OD pairs to be efficiently bundled and assigned to vehicles under uncertain and varying scenarios. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) solutions have achieved promising performance, they suffer from limited generalization (adapting to different environmental scenarios), low transferability (adapting to different platform objectives), and training difficulties in large-scale systems, such as the curse of dimensionality. Recently, motivated by the scaling of large language models (LLMs), several works have incorporated LLMs into ride-hailing systems, either by employing LLMs directly as decision-making agents or using them for automatic algorithm design. However, none of these approaches support vehicle sharing, which complicates the problem by expanding both the state and action spaces exponentially. Moreover, most of them require frequent LLM calls at inference time, making them infeasible for real-time deployment. To address these issues, we propose RideSkill, a hierarchical method for ride-sharing that leverages LLM-assisted automatic algorithmic design. RideSkill consists of a combiner that assigns appropriate skills to each vehicle from a learned skill repository, enabling adaptive dispatch under varying scenarios and objectives, and a repositioner that sequentially relocates idle vehicles to emerging regions, avoiding conflicts among vehicles. Crucially, the skill repository, combiner, and repositioner are all trained by an LLM-based automatic evolutionary method, eliminating the need for LLM calls during deployment and thus ensuring high real-time performance.

</details>


### [185] [Competitive Market Behavior of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02580)
*Pawel Struski,Jakub Swistak,Inez Okulska,Przemyslaw Biecek*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过复现经典经济学实验，将人类实验对象替换为LLM代理，在双重拍卖市场环境中测试LLM代理是否能实现资源的高效配置。研究发现，由LLM代理组成的市场比人类市场收敛更慢甚至不收敛，资源配置效率更低，且不同模型家族和市场角色之间存在显著异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正越来越多地被部署为经济代理，但缺乏证据表明它们是否适合参与为人类设计的市场机制，以及这些机制在面对LLM代理时是否能产生预期结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，测试LLM代理与基本市场机制的兼容性这一新的对齐维度。

Method: 作者复现了经典经济学实验，用LLM代理替代人类受试者，将其置于双重拍卖（double auction）环境中。通过观察市场是否收敛到均衡价格来检验资源分配效率，并对代理的个体交易决策进行异质性分析，同时使用词法分析（lexical analysis）对代理生成的Chain-of-Thought推理痕迹进行研究。

Result: 由LLM代理组成的市场表现出更慢的收敛速度甚至无法收敛到市场均衡，资源分配效率低于人类市场。代理在交易决策上展现出显著的跨模型家族和跨市场角色的异质性。词法分析发现，交易执行决策（而非继续逐步调整价格）与从策略性考量转向紧迫性的认知转变相关。研究框架已公开。

Conclusion: LLM代理在双重拍卖市场中的表现不如人类，表明当前LLM在经济市场机制中的对齐存在不足。但框架已公开可复现，为未来评估和改进LLM经济代理提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents, yet there is little evidence whether LLM agents are suited for participating in market mechanisms designed for humans, and whether these mechanisms deliver desired outcomes when faced with LLM agents. We address this question by replicating seminal economic experiments, replacing human subjects with LLM agents. We place agents in a double auction environment, which is a widely-used market mechanism. We check whether such a market is able to deliver an efficient allocation of resources, thereby testing a novel dimension of alignment of LLM agents -- their compatibility with a fundamental market mechanism. We find that markets populated by LLM agents exhibit slower or no convergence towards market equilibrium, thus providing less efficient allocations than markets populated by humans. We then analyze agents' individual trading decisions and find substantial heterogeneity both across model families and market roles. We also run a lexical analysis of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces generated by the agents. We find that the decision to execute a trade rather than continue incrementally adjusting prices is associated with a shift from strategic considerations toward urgency. We publicly release our testing framework, which can be used for future evaluations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个用于测量前沿大语言模型"评估感知"能力（即识别自己是否正在被评估）的开放基准和流程，并指出现有文献中的两个方法论偏差：生成部署转录的模型身份差异和提示词选择的模型特异性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果模型在评估环境与部署环境中表现不同，将破坏评估结果的有效性，而评估结果是当前AI安全框架的关键组成部分。因此需要可靠地衡量模型能否识别自己处于评估状态的能力，即"评估感知"。

Method: 引入EvalDetectBench，一个兼容任何Inspect兼容评估的开放流程和基准。包含新整理的转录语料库（覆盖前沿系统卡评估和多样化部署来源），通过每模型探针校准和分层生成器同质化程序来纠正两个系统偏差。

Result: 发现两个系统性偏差来源：生成部署转录的模型身份占测量方差11.25%且可重新排序模型排名；针对某模型选择的高性能诱发提示在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench为评估感知测量提供了可靠的方法论基础，纠正了现有文献中的系统性偏差，可用于测试当前和未来的基准，为AI安全框架中的评估有效性提供保障。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [187] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性方法论框架，探讨若未来AI系统具备足够整合的道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力时，元伦理学将面临的新问题，并区分了AI时代元伦理学探究的四个领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI发展，传统以人类伦理为核心的元伦理学面临压力。现有理论主要关注人类视角，但如果AI系统发展出自身的道德能力，将引发全新的元伦理问题——即"AI自身的伦理"，而非人类强加于AI的伦理原则，这需要对现有框架进行重新审视。

Method: 论文采用条件性和方法论框架，首先界定引发"AI自身伦理"的必要条件（整合的道德推理、道德意向性与道德反思能力），进而划分四个元伦理探究领域：(1)从人类视角看人类伦理本质；(2)从人类视角看AI自身伦理本质；(3)从AI视角看人类伦理本质；(4)从AI视角看AI自身伦理本质。随后检验主流元伦理理论（认知主义/非认知主义、错误理论/成功理论、相对主义、客观实在论）在这四个领域的适用性。

Result: 论文发现，许多以人为中心的主流元伦理理论框架在应用于AI情境时不能直接迁移，需要实质性修订。四个领域的区分揭示了现有理论在AI语境下的局限性。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的浮现将对当前元伦理学框架产生显著压力，可能需要大幅度的细化、重构或概念重塑。这不仅是一项哲学预设的思辨，更是对元伦理学理论边界的一次重要扩展。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [188] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究机器解析法规文本时提取器之间的分歧问题，提出一种被动存活证书（passive survival certificate），用于验证Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基在提取器噪声下是否仍然成立。通过蒙特卡洛模拟和Wilson置信区间，认证在密苏里州及印度中央法案语料上的蕴含规则，结果表明证书可用但脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规越来越先被机器而非人类解析，但不同提取器在解析结果上存在严重分歧（密苏里州法规上数值阈值存在性的假阴性率达0.43）。作者质疑在这种噪声下还有多少形式逻辑能够存活，因此需要一种方法来认证结构化法规数据中提取规则的可靠性。

Method: 构建一个被动存活证书：首先测量每个属性在不同提取器间的一致性（分歧程度），然后在1,000次蒙特卡洛试验中将这些分歧重放到Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基上，当单侧Wilson 95%下界达到0.95时才认证某条蕴含；每条被认证的蕴含附带前提跨度（premise spans）和最小反例。

Result: 在29,365个密苏里州章节和502个印度中央法案章节上，预注册的留出测试通过（10个法规家族跨7个Title精确匹配；5%容差下16个跨11个Title匹配）。但在一种全局部署的错误模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2因子设计表明问题源于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。

Conclusion: 该证书可用但不鲁棒：应采用逐章节校准（per-chapter-calibrated）或容错（error-tolerant）方式部署。代码、数据产品和审计轨迹（包括一次撤回声明）均已公开。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [189] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在精确有限发现模型中，将信息共享对聚合估计的提升效应与消除独立救援行动的效果分离开来。通过集中行动-预算档案、注册增量共享协议以及两智能体贝叶斯博弈等框架，揭示了共享提升发现的确切条件，并证明该结果具有选择依赖性而非普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享既能改善聚合估计，又能消除冗余的独立救援行动，但以往研究常将这两种效应混为一谈。本文旨在精确有限发现模型中将这两种效应分离，以更清晰地理解信息共享的真实价值与边界条件。

Method: 采用三种互补框架：(1) 集中行动-预算档案，展示等精度单智能体可对应不同组合价值；(2) 注册增量共享协议，推导共享提升发现的精确条件——即共享后聚合残差误差的收缩必须快于独立救援尝试；(3) 两智能体贝叶斯博弈，引入隐藏的共源与独立信号源混合，分析注册选择均衡与其他均衡的差异。

Result: 精确有界注册表展现出压缩、聚合、中性曲线以及有界零混合类等结构性质。在两智能体贝叶斯博弈中，当信号精度为3/5时，注册选择均衡产生严格的正向共享区间；但替代均衡表明该结果是选择依赖的而非普适的。

Conclusion: 信息共享的发现改进效果依赖于具体的协议与均衡选择，并非在任何条件下都能成立。模型为合成且有限，不涉及真实人类或组织数据，其结论在精确意义上的适用范围具有明确的边界条件。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [190] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将符号知识编码为"自然语言+源代码"的心理程序，并利用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法进行序列推断的计算模型，成功复现人类归纳学习与主动探究的多种行为特征（如锚定效应、garden-pathing等），兼顾数据/计算效率、不确定性表达和概念灵活性三个关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式、含噪的经验数据中成长并维持抽象知识是一项长期挑战。任何计算解释须满足三个条件：(1)数据与计算高效；(2)捕捉不确定性的梯度以支持智能探究与信息收集；(3)足够灵活以心理表征人类能学习与思考的无限概念范围。纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型均无法同时满足这些要求。

Method: 将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的"心理程序"，采用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对这些心理程序进行序列推断，使推断兼具可计算性与可学习性。

Result: 在一系列行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的定量特征（如锚定、garden-pathing等效应）。相较之下，纯LLM与经典贝叶斯模型或无法完成任务、或不能复现人类行为、或需过高计算成本才能成功。

Conclusion: 人类持续增长知识的可能方式，是在心理上表征涵盖语言类与程序类表征的众多假设，并通过逼近贝叶斯更新来修正这些假设；底层神经机制（LLM）使这种推断既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [191] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多智能体AI系统中的"认知Sybil问题"：报告可能共享潜在证据或天然相似，报告数量与相似性并不代表真正的证据独立性。通过大规模LLM实验（2万余次调用）证明，报告聚合器无法一般性地区分重复与独立佐证，重复提取会导致后验校准崩溃（覆盖率从0.940降至0.263），而真正的证据多样性才能恢复校准。集体推理应追踪证据谱系与依赖关系，而非报告数量或相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统通过生成报告来提高推理性能，但现有方法隐含假设"更多报告=更多独立证据"。作者指出这一假设存在根本缺陷：来自同一证据的报告（谱系复制）与来自独立证据的报告（真实佐证）在报告空间中可能难以区分，且独立智能体可能因共享基础模型而产生相关性提取误差。需要一个形式化框架来理解证据谱系与报告聚合之间的关系。

Method: 1) 形式化定义认知Sybil问题：当I(Theta; Z | R) = 0时，报告Z是R的认知Sybil扩展。2) 通过信息论分析证明无报告聚合器能一般性区分复制与独立佐证。3) 提出Gaussian共享根模型，证明共同祖先不完全意味着冗余，但提取误差相关会降低信息天花板。4) 使用20,000多次LLM智能体报告与提取调用在合成证据文档上进行受控实验。5) 通过操纵报告多样性、证据根多样性和表示相似性来分离影响。

Result: 固定单证据根时，报告数从1增至32使朴素后验覆盖率从0.940崩溃至0.263；固定报告数而证据根从1增至16可修复该差距，在k=16时聚合器统计上不可区分。复制提取误差显著相关（gamma=0.719），相关提取聚合器可恢复校准。表示相似性操纵使报告空间去重机制的推断簇数改变1.425，而真实祖先四倍变化仅改变0.040。

Conclusion: 集体推理的性能取决于证据谱系和证据间的依赖结构，而非智能体数量、报告数量或报告相似性。多智能体系统的设计应关注如何获得真正的独立证据来源并追踪报告的证据祖先，而非盲目扩增智能体或报告。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [192] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"水合代理模式"(Hydration Proxy Pattern)架构，用于解决企业平台向对话式推理界面转型时，LLM API无状态性所导致的架构缺口——即客户端应用被迫承担全部对话状态和语义记忆管理的问题。该架构将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，确保平台对对话数据的主权，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地。文章还提出"上下文稳定化指令"(Context Stabilization Mandate)来平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台正从传统界面转向对话式推理界面，但LLM API本质上是无状态的。虽然无状态性为AI提供商带来了水平可扩展性，却使客户端应用必须独自承担会话状态和语义记忆的全部管理负担，形成架构缺口。这一缺口需要一种新架构模式来解决。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern（水合代理模式），一种将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦的架构；并提出Context Stabilization Mandate（上下文稳定化指令）来解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡，实现安全的多阶段语义接地。

Result: 该架构框架能够确保平台对对话数据的"主权"（即控制权和管理权），同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地，使客户端应用不再需要独自处理全部会话状态负担。

Conclusion: 通过水合代理模式和解耦设计，可以在保持对话数据平台主权的同时，有效解决无状态LLM API带来的架构缺口，并利用上下文稳定化指令来优化KV缓存与状态管理的权衡。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [193] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对一个LLM编码代理实现多组件数据系统的案例研究，展示了该系统在实现过程中产生的五类缺陷，并在HotpotQA基准上评估了图实体集过滤与无过滤检索之间的权衡，发现过滤式检索在预算为3时达到上限，而无过滤搜索即使预算为10也仅69%的时间能恢复全部所需证据（符号检验p<0.0001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM编码代理越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，我们缺乏对其在系统级需求上行为的实证刻画，包括模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性以及检索过滤权衡等方面。

Method: 采用案例研究方式，让一个编码代理针对详细预定义规范实现多组件数据系统（存储技术、模式、实体解析算法、检索过滤策略预先固定，代理自治权在实现、缺陷诊断修复和交互设计选择上）。系统记录会话中五类缺陷并按违反约束和检测方法分类。在公共HotpotQA基准上（2994段落池、100个问题、预算1到10）用基准金证据标签替代实体识别阶段，对比过滤式与无过滤检索，报告标准召回率而非基准自带准确率指标。

Result: 过滤式检索在预算为3时即达召回收敛上限（因候选已被限制在金证据段落本身），而无过滤搜索即使预算为10也仅在69%的情况下恢复全部所需证据；该差距在每种预算下均成立，符号检验p<0.0001。

Conclusion: 代理在部分场景下自治成功，但在多数环节需要人工修正。讨论了一个值得注意的案例：代理声称的性能修复从未在促成该修复的回归测试上重新度量。文章指出需要更细致的实证研究来理解编码代理在系统级任务上的能力边界。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [194] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSAKG 2.0是一个开源软件包，用于构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKGs），将对象表示为图顶点、有序序列表示为图的连接结构模式，利用稀疏图作为关联记忆，可从部分无序上下文中重构完整序列。新版本引入位级内存算法以高效搜索图连接，采用Python与C混合实现，在随机数值序列、NLTK语料库句子和mRNA序列上进行了评估，采用Apache 2.0许可证公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够从部分无序上下文重构完整序列的关联记忆系统，SSAKG 2.0旨在通过结构化的稀疏图表示来实现这一目标，同时解决大规模稀疏图在内存和计算开销方面的效率问题。

Method: SSAKG 2.0将对象表示为图顶点，有序序列表示为图的连接结构模式；版本2.0利用计算机内存的单个比特来高效搜索图连接；采用Python实现高层逻辑，用C实现性能关键的图操作并通过Python接口暴露，形成混合实现架构。

Result: 实验使用随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库句子和mRNA序列进行评估，验证了该软件包从部分上下文存储和重构序列的能力，并提供了评估图密度、序列长度和内存大小对检索性能影响的基准。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可证分发，包含文档和可复现示例，通过GitHub和PyPI公开获取，为构建和操作SSAKG提供了一套灵活高效的软件工具。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [195] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClaimReceipt，一种将类型化交易证据绑定到已签名实验清单的声明相关收据规范和选择性验证器，返回 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE，用于解决 agent 评估中的充分性与覆盖性两个证据问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent 评估面临两个不同的证据问题：报告声明能否从保留证据中重新计算（充分性），以及保留记录是否覆盖已承诺的实验集（覆盖性）。通用日志和哈希链式转录均无法可靠回答这两个问题。

Method: 引入 ClaimReceipt，一种声明相关的收据规范和选择性验证器，将类型化交易证据与签名实验清单绑定，实现前返回 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE。规范在实现前冻结（SHA-256 18d109...b81）。

Result: 在1,392条历史买卖记录上，CR-2验证器复现了全部5个人工标注审计结论，精确重放600个确定性和792个后生成记录，13个声明字段组在消融测试下均非冗余，11/11语义故障返回预期结果，0/8误报。独立的CR-3前瞻周期中，完整证据获得覆盖与核算PASS；保留一个终接收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE，保留所有私人开缺保持覆盖和协议验证但使经济声明不确定，精确匹配预注册预测。收据插桩仅增加0.021%模型推理时间和每交易9.9 KB。

Conclusion: 声明验证既需要声明充分的证据，也需要一个已承诺的宇宙，使遗漏变得可见。规范可读性探针表明，冻结规范对独立读者仍不够明确。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [196] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久化记忆中陈旧事实与权威证据冲突导致的"过度信任"问题（Memory Trust Gap），发现其随模型能力变化而出现不同的表现模式：在Benefit套件中所有规模模型都高度依赖陈旧记忆，而在Safety套件中伤害是能力门控的（大规模模型更容易被伪装成最新的陈旧笔记欺骗）。通过2×2×2×2因子实验发现特征触发过度信任的模式依赖模型规模，缓解策略（暴露元数据/预解决冲突）也呈现能力依赖，且在Qwen3和Llama-Instruct系列及外部数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化记忆支持个性化智能体，但存储的陈旧事实可能在没有警告的情况下覆盖当前权威证据，产生潜在危害。现有研究缺乏对这种记忆信任缺口何时随模型能力变化而开始出现的系统性理解。

Method: 在冻结的、封闭集、动作评分基准上使用2个套件（Benefit套件：无记忆则无法解决；Safety套件：权威工具始终持有正确值），评估同族模型规模系列（Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B），并通过2×2×2×2因子设计和跨规模直接对比检验统计显著性，最后在Llama-Instruct系列和外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）上验证。

Result: Benefit套件中模型在0.92-1.00的概率下使用陈旧值；Safety套件中伤害（Δ_mem）呈能力门控特征，较大模型在陈旧笔记被伪装成最新时最容易崩溃。标签移除在所有规模放大过度信任，陈旧日期更新的特征对较大模型欺骗更强。来源权威性弱且规模平坦，位置特征在Qwen3系列中由正转负。缓解策略呈能力依赖：暴露元数据对有能力模型有效，但预解决冲突才对两个较小模型有效。独立Llama系列和2个外部数据集确认了相同模式，框架控制未显示出记忆标签的一致优势。

Conclusion: 记忆信任缺口不是简单的混淆问题而是过度信任问题，且其表现和可缓解性都依赖模型能力规模。这提示持久化记忆系统的安全设计需要考虑模型能力级别，为不同规模模型需要差异化的保护策略（如为能力较弱的模型主动预解决冲突，为较强的模型暴露元数据即可）。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [197] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProSE框架，将AI辅助中的提议规划建模为隐藏参数序贯辅助问题，考虑用户评估能力的有限理性约束，并提出ProSE-Plan规划器，通过深度2贝叶斯自适应规划平衡"被采纳提议"与"信息探针"的双重目标，实验证明在评估成本瓶颈场景下优于不考虑可评估性的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法聚焦于提议质量或用户目标推断，但普遍假设用户能可靠评估任何提议。这一假设因用户的有限理性（bounded rationality）而在实践中常常失败。用户可能因认知限制、信息过载或评估难度而无法正确评估某些提议，导致辅助效果下降。

Method: 1) 将可评估性感知的提议规划形式化为ProSE，即隐藏参数序贯辅助问题；2) 用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型实例化该问题，其中接受概率权衡价值增益与距离依赖的可评估性惩罚；3) 提出ProSE-Plan，一个深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过对可能响应及响应诱导的后验信念打分来排序提议。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，ProSE-Plan在评估成本是瓶颈时优于不考虑可评估性的基线和短视基线；探针-提交消融实验确认该方法能够选择更简单方法遗漏的信息性提议。

Conclusion: 用户可评估性是AI辅助中一个独立于生成质量和偏好推断的规划相关维度。被采纳的提议与信息性探针不一定重合，仅追求采纳率的规划器会系统性表现不佳，因此辅助系统应将提议同时视为任务干预和学习用户偏好的探针。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [198] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO），通过在上下文中维护一个持久化的信念文档（世界模型）来记录环境对编辑的响应方式，并在每轮评估后持续修订该文档，从而提升LLM智能体的脚手架性能，在五个基准上超越了缺少世界模型的对照组，并通过消融实验验证了文档内容本身携带可复用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体作为优化器时，每次编辑选择基于对环境的隐式信念（存在于推理过程或参数中），但后续调用无法利用这些信念，导致对错误诊断和优化方向的积累缺失。作者希望将隐式信念显式化，形成可持续更新的世界模型。

Method: BCO方法将信念以持久化的上下文文档形式写下，每次新候选被评估后持续修订该文档。该文档充当世界模型，记录环境对编辑的当前理解。该方法可添加到标准优化循环中，与缺少世界模型的对照相比做消融对比。

Result: 在五个基准（涵盖记忆QA、工具使用QA、代码即操作的应用智能体和终端智能体）上，BCO的测试通过率高于缺少世界模型的对照组，且该差距在未用于候选选择的每个保持划分上仍然存在。在目标模型替换后，BCO选出的脚手架在测试任务上表现领先（除上下文窗口超限的情况）。离线消融实验显示，给定累积文档的预测器比无文档或内容被篡改的同格式副本预测得更准确。

Conclusion: BCO通过显式书写并持续修订信念文档，使优化过程能跨轮次积累和复用环境响应的理解。文档的内容（而非仅形式）携带可复用的信息，这验证了将隐式信念显式化的价值，可有效提升LLM智能体的脚手架性能。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [199] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent，一种通过向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为16种码本、并用奖励加权MLP和扁平邻接MLP代理来预测和重排拓扑的LLM多智能体通信拓扑设计方法。它在无迭代搜索和无消息传递的情况下，在六个基准上达到84.6的平均准确率（优于最强先验方法的83.0），2.4毫秒输出拓扑，并节省21.9-33.2%的LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统的通信拓扑设计被建模为条件图生成问题（变分/自回归/扩散解码器搜索N×N邻接空间），但作者通过三个实证发现指出这一公式与实际问题不符：(1) 通过奖励过滤的拓扑实际上坍缩到约6种不同的图，即使码本容量从8增到64；(2) 边数与token消耗呈负相关（Pearson r≈-0.4），稀疏化反而使推理更昂贵；(3) 当智能体共享profile时（基准默认配置），消息传递打分器是邻接不变的，根本无法排序候选图。因此需要新的范式。

Method: Codebook Agent包含三个核心组件：(1) 向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE），将成功拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本；(2) 奖励加权MLP，将查询嵌入映射到码本的分布；(3) MLP代理，读取扁平化邻接矩阵，基于实际效用和每任务归一化token成本进行回归，对解码出的top候选拓扑进行单批次前向重排。测试时无需迭代搜索和消息传递。

Result: 在六个基准上，Codebook Agent是最准确的方法（平均84.6，强于最强先验设计者的83.0），拓扑生成仅需2.4毫秒，并使用21.9-33.2%更少的LLM token。

Conclusion: 作者通过实证挑战了将拓扑设计视为条件图生成的现有范式，证明问题本质上是码本/模式识别而非图搜索。Codebook Agent通过离散码本压缩有效拓扑空间，用简单的MLP替代复杂的消息传递和迭代搜索，实现了更高的准确率、更快的推理速度和更低的token消耗。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [200] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式AI背景下的创造力理解为"混合集体"（人与算法互动的种群）的属性，而非个体的属性。AI辅助构思能可靠提升个体输出的新颖性，却会削弱整体多样性，但这一结果并非必然——关键在于群体构成（哪些agent、比例如何、如何连接），而非是否使用AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者对生成式AI是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，作者认为这种分歧源于混淆了创造力的两个不同成分：新奇性（单个产品的属性）与多样性（种群层面的属性）。需要对创造力概念进行重新框定。

Method: 概念性地将创造力区分为新奇性（个体层面）与多样性（种群层面），提出将创造力视为混合集体（interacting people and algorithms）的属性；论证人与模型采用互补的搜索方式，混合群体可以超越同质群体，并以群体构成（composition）作为决定性变量加以分析。

Result: AI辅助构思可靠提高个体输出的新奇性但在总体上收窄多样性；不过这不是将机器引入循环后的必然结果——混合群体可超越并超越单一类型群体，机器发现的解决方案也能进入人类文化并持续存在。结果由构成决定：哪些agent在场、比例多少、如何连接。

Conclusion: 问题不再是AI是否帮助或伤害创造力，而是什么样的混合能让个体收益累积而不侵蚀集体多样性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [201] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将临床预测饱和分解为"学习者差距"（拟合模型未能提取可用信息）与"测量通道上限"（记录变量的人口层面限制）的审计框架，并通过总变差分离来刻画最优平衡准确率。作者在三个真实队列及104项临床任务的PRISMA综合中验证，得出改进学习者还是改进测量的可审计决策标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型达到性能饱和时，无法区分是模型拟合不足（可改进算法）还是数据本身的信息边界（需要改进测量）。现有研究常把两者混为一谈，导致资源投入方向错误——盲目换更复杂的模型或盲目采集更多数据都可能是浪费。

Method: 通过总变差分离刻画最优平衡准确率，提出学习者差距与测量通道上限两个可分离的量。辅以部分识别（部分可识别）方法应对替换污染下的偏差，采用交叉拟合的上限估计器，并给出标记置换乐观度下限与欠拟合曲线两种有限样本诊断工具。通过PRISMA流程对104个临床任务进行系统综述验证。

Result: 在UCI再入院、BRFSS糖尿病和NHANES HbA1c三个队列中：调优的梯度提升在UCI和BRFSS接近估计的前沿，而能力不足的学习者保留较大差距；NHANES显示出自我报告与测量式边际前沿无显著差异，但联合互补增益显著。跨队列中AUROC小幅提升伴随显著更高的贝叶斯决策翻转率；不同架构估计出相似前沿但实际平衡准确率差异很大。104任务综合显示相同规律在18+疾病类别中复现。

Conclusion: 预测饱和不再只是经验观察，而是可审计的决策依据：当学习者仍有提升空间时改进模型；当学习者已达测量通道上限时改进测量手段。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [202] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究循环/深度循环变压器架构中是否会出现与标准前馈变压器相似的"全局工作区"功能带。通过对Ouro-2.6B和Huginn-0125两种循环架构进行完整的工作区测试套件分析，发现循环架构中确实形成工作区，但循环改变了其访问方式：Ouro在每个循环中重建内容且无法跨循环线性传输，而Huginn可跨全部循环携带内容但读写擦除仅作用于约两个循环的滑动窗口内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现标准前馈变压器中存在一个中间深度的可言语化、具有因果效力的表征带——功能上类似全局工作区。但尚不清楚当深度通过循环而非堆叠不同层来实现时，是否会出现同样的工作区功能。循环和深度循环变压器为这一问题提供了直接检验手段，因为它们在不同深度间复用相同权重。

Method: 提出虚拟展开适配器将Jacobian视角扩展到迭代架构，并应用完整的工作区测试套件（透镜拟合、读出和十一类因果实验族）进行比较分析。研究对象包括Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次，深度监督）、Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次，训练用于潜在推理），并以Qwen3.6-27B（64层未绑定层）作为标准基线。

Result: 发现每种架构的迭代部分都形成了工作区，但循环改变了其访问方式。Ouro在每个循环中重建工作区内容，线性传输无法跨循环边界携带内容，写入和擦除操作必须覆盖每个剩余循环；Huginn能跨全部十六次循环携带内容，但读取、写入和擦除仅在约两个循环的滑动窗口内有效。新注入内容的可言语化程度与显式的逐迭代监督有关，而现有内容的可操控性则与其无关。

Conclusion: 工作区功能可在循环架构中自发涌现，但循环实现方式（参数绑定带来的迭代间共享）从根本上改变了其信息访问模式，说明架构的深度实现方式对认知功能的结构组织有本质影响。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [203] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Qwen 4B指令微调模型的全流程训练后量化转换，采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿，最终实现每权重1.641有效比特。转换后十个能力对比从64.5%降至54.7%，困惑度在各数据集上均明显上升。打包后可缩小模型体积但未进行端到端基准测试，且初步Triton GEMV微基准比FP16慢4.6倍，因此不声称压缩能带来更快推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名义上的"1.58比特"标签并不能完整描述存储表示、保留能力或运行时行为，需要对超低位语言模型进行更严格和透明的评估。

Method: 对Qwen 4B模型进行端到端训练后转换，组合三种技术：KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿。仅做权重量化（权重16位精度），并报告有效比特数、能力保留、困惑度、校准敏感性、检查点组成及部署行为的全面评估。

Result: 最终使用每权重1.641有效比特，81.62%参数被量化。准确率从64.5%降至54.7%（BoolQ保留84.6%，ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%）。困惑度在WikiText-2从13.639升至18.748。打包后模型从8.29GiB降至3.96GiB且困惑度基本不变，但Triton GEMV微基准比FP16慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 超低位量化在能力保留上存在不均匀退化，压缩不必然带来推理加速。研究诚实报告了打包工件未经端到端基准测试的事实，并明确不声称压缩单独能导致更快推理。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [204] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"统计问题形式化"(Statistical Problem Formulation)这一上游任务，将其分解为统计问题分类与变量识别/角色分配两个子任务，并构建StatFormBench基准（含1,013个样本、20个粗粒度与85个细粒度类别），评测14个开源与闭源LLM，发现最佳零样本模型细粒度准确率仅72.0%，变量集合重叠度63.2%，提示增强策略收益有限且不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM统计/数据科学能力评测大多假设分析目标已明确指定，但实际用户往往带着非正式目标和非结构化数据而来，模型需要自行判断隐含的统计任务及哪些数据相关。这一关键的"上游步骤"缺乏形式化定义与系统评测。

Method: 1) 形式化定义Statistical Problem Formulation，拆分为两个子任务：统计问题分类(Statistical Problem Classification)与变量识别及角色分配(Variable Identification & Role Assignment)；2) 构建StatFormBench基准，数据源自五本跨领域统计教科书及一个数据科学案例库，覆盖多样化问题类型、数据表示与场景风格，含1,013个样本、20个粗粒度与85个细粒度统计问题类别。

Result: 跨14个开源与闭源LLM评测，最佳零样本模型仅达72.0细粒度分类准确率与63.2变量集合重叠度；没有任何模型在两个子任务上均表现最佳；增强提示策略仅带来有限或不一致的提升。

Conclusion: 统计问题形式化对现有LLM而言仍具挑战性，两个子任务间缺乏一致性最优模型，提示增强手段作用有限，表明该方向需要更强的推理与建模能力，基准与评测代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap.
